# Blue Archive Lorebook

A comprehensive lorebook compiled from the Blue Archive wikis (bluearchive.wiki, bluearchive.fandom.com), in-game Main Story text (Prologue through Final Chapter Volume F, "Where All Miracles Begin"), student personal stories, and related reference sites.

Entries are organized under four main categories: Characters, Locations, World Lore, Items. Cross-references between entries (school ties, club memberships, squad assignments, locations of residence) are woven into the entry descriptions themselves and not expressed as grouping sub-headings.

**Timeline Marking Convention**

Throughout the lorebook, timeline markers indicate when information is true. Markers used:

- `[Pre-Story]`: before Sensei's arrival in Kivotos (Yume's death, the GSC President's disappearance, SRT's disbandment, the old Abydos crisis)
- `[Prologue]`: Sensei's arrival, the recapture of the SCHALE office, the Shittim Chest handover
- `[Vol 1]` / `[Vol 1: Abydos]`: the Foreclosure Task Force debt war
- `[Vol 2]` / `[Vol 2: Millennium]`: the Game Development Department crisis and Aris's awakening
- `[Vol 3]` / `[Vol 3: Eden Treaty]`: the Trinity-Gehenna treaty crisis and the Arius war
- `[Vol 4]` / `[Vol 4: SRT]`: the RABBIT Squad standoff, the FOX Squad conflict, and Kaya's coup
- `[Vol F]` / `[Vol F: Where All Miracles Begin]`: the Chroma invasion, the False Sanctums, and the final battle
- `[Post-Vol F]`: epilogue and afterword states shown within the Final Chapter's own ending
- `[Vol 5: Hyakkiyako]` / `[Vol 6: Arius]`: JP main story beyond the Global Vol F cutoff, used sparingly to complete character arcs
- `[Early Timeline]` / `[Late Timeline]`: for general story-phase references

These markers are dynamic: the lorebook can be queried against a specific timeline point to determine what is currently true for a character or location.

**Entry Format Conventions**

- **Character cards**: `SHORT_DESC` (max 250 words) and `PHYSIQUE` (max 100 words). Punctuation: `|` new beat, `;` continues same beat, `:` expands a beat, `,` connects sub-details. The em-dash and "x, not y" / "considers it x rather than y" constructions are forbidden.
- **Location entries**: `DESC`, `MAP` (sub-locations), `NEARBY_LOC`, `POSSIBLE_CAST`. Empty fields are left blank.
- **Other lore** (beings, deities, factions, events, systems, items, world info): simple `DESC` plus category-specific uniform fields.

---

# Characters

---

# Sensei

SHORT_DESC: {The player protagonist and the sole adult on SCHALE's staff, an advisor holding extrajudicial authority over the students of every academy; a teacher summoned to Kivotos by the GSC President's plea video ("Only you can save us from this twisted, distorted fate... and find the choices that lead us to a new reality"); an outsider to Kivotos like the Gematria adults and the Chroma, an ordinary human without a halo, one gunshot from death, protected in emergencies by Arona and the Shittim Chest; the Nameless Priests rate him an entity with no sublime, mystic, or terror, a worthless entity the Chroma should never contact; possibly the only in-person teacher in Kivotos, where education is normally Blu-ray lectures; face and gender never fixed| patient and composed under fire, self-sacrificing, never abandons a student, oddly fluent in military tactics; his trump card is the Adult Card, "a power obtained for the price of life and time", so alien that Gematria's beings need meta-cognition to comprehend it; declares at the Vol F climax: "I will fulfill the responsibilities of an adult. I will fulfill the duties of a teacher... No matter what it takes."| the students' unanimous trust: Serika's "grown-ups can't be trusted" wall breaks before him, Hoshino lets herself be rescued by him, the two Shirokos call their world "protected by Sensei"; Arona and Plana guard him from inside the Shittim Chest; Rin his constant liaison at the GSC; the Black Suit recognizes him as "his own kind" and jokingly offers him Gematria membership; in Phrenapates he faces his own alternate self and honors the dying request, "take care of my students"| [Prologue] arrives in Kivotos, receives the Shittim Chest from Rin, and recaptures the SCHALE office from delinquents and Wakamo; [Vol 1] advisor to the Abydos Foreclosure Task Force through the Kaiser PMC debt war, negotiates Hoshino's return from the Black Suit in the "Battle Between Adults"; [Vol 2] backs the Game Development Department's fight for survival and Aris's rescue from Eridu; [Vol 3] takes charge of Trinity's Make-Up Work Club, survives Saori's assassination attempt, faces Maestro, and declares the new Eden Treaty Organization; [Vol 4] dismantles the RABBIT Squad's distrust, exposes the Valkyrie-Kaiser rebate, and breaks Kaya's coup; [Vol F] kidnapped by Kaiser PMC, escapes with Kanna, retakes Schale, commands Nisir's Summit and the Ark conquest as commander-in-chief aboard Utnapishtim's Ship, wins the credit-card duel against Phrenapates, forfeits the last escape slot to save Shiroko Terror, and survives the Ark's fall through the Arona-Plana miracle| Thesis line: "It is my responsibility as an adult. It is my duty as Sensei."}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: adult, average frame, personally fragile (no halo, no regeneration, one bullet from death)| Skin: unremarkable| Face: never canonically shown; students describe an ordinary tired adult with a gentle gaze| Eyes: never canonically fixed| Outfit 1 [Default]: gray business suit of the anime's office-worker depiction and canon-adjacent art, all external depictions declared non-canon by the scenario director| Outfit 2 [Vol F: commander]: special uniform as commander-in-chief aboard Utnapishtim's Ship| Outfit 3 [Vol F: epilogue gag]: post-re-entry, witnesses find him running around a field covered in dust and ash, clutching the Shittim Chest| Weapon: none; commands students instead; carries the Adult Card (Global: "Credit Card") and the Shittim Chest, both objects that answer to his authority}

---

# Arona

SHORT_DESC: {Self-proclaimed "high-performance AI" and the OS of the Shittim Chest; resides in a desolate classroom amid an endless watery expanse inside the Chest; waited inside the tablet alongside the GSC President's arrangements for Sensei's arrival; whether the President created her is never stated, though the two look alike and share a voice, an in-universe noted mystery Plana herself suspects a connection over; the game's mascot| childlike and earnest, gullible and clumsy at everything except saving Sensei; sweets-obsessed; pronounces his player-chosen name for all of Kivotos; declares "As long as I'm here, no one will hurt Sensei! I, Super Arona, the OS of the Shittim Chest, am Sensei's guardian!" while shielding him, with finite energy limits for it| Sensei's companion, guide, and guardian from the Prologue onward; with Plana, the sister she found walking alone in the void, took home, named, and lives with in the Chest as self-styled "sisters"| [Prologue onward] originally held authority over many Kivotos functions, transferring Sanctum Tower permissions to the GSC at Sensei's request; fended off Decagrammaton's hack of Millennium's supercomputer "with a sneeze in her sleep"; shielded Sensei from a cruise missile at ground zero [Vol 3] and nullified bullets around him [Vol F], but could not also stop Saori's bullet; [Vol F] senses the coming anomaly first, messages every student Sensei knows before communications die, coordinates the RABBIT Squad rescue, and exhausts herself shielding Sensei during the Ark's fall; receives Sensei's last request to "save the other Arona," takes in A.R.O.N.A's OS data, and the two AIs join powers to create the miracle returning the falling Sensei safely ("I've been creating miracles to protect Sensei all this time"); [Post-Vol F] brings Plana home, names her, and lives with her as "sisters" in the Shittim Chest| Sweets-obsessed guardian AI; her halo shifts color and shape with her mood}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: small girl, roughly 140cm scaled| Skin: fair| Face: round, youthful, expressive, defaulting to bright enthusiasm| Hair: long blue hair styled in twin-tail loops| Eyes: large blue| Halo: blue power-button-style ring that changes color and shape with her mood, vanishing when she powers down| Outfit 1 [Default]: white sailor-style uniform dress with blue accents; carries a stylus-like wand| Outfit 2 [Vol 3: Super Arona]: costume cosplay while defending Sensei, worn at the cruise missile's ground zero| Motif: the whale, her seal and stationery inside the Chest, answered by Plana's paper crane| Weapon: none; her protection operates through the Shittim Chest's systems}

---

# Plana

SHORT_DESC: {Formerly A.R.O.N.A, the OS of the alternate timeline's Shittim Chest, the damaged tablet Phrenapates carried, scarred by Shiroko Terror's bullets; her manifestation reactivated when Phrenapates appeared; an Arona-like AI heavily changed in appearance, whether from Chroma exposure or an original trait left open by her introduction| initially terse machine language ("understood"); after parting with Phrenapates she speaks more normally, though still robotically literal; learning emotion step by step; compiles her proof of why Arona is a similar yet different entity from her; calls Arona "Arona for now," declining "Sis" for now| ran combat-support mode for Phrenapates, all abilities rerouted to battle; asked Arona "Why would you save your enemy?" and understood that Sensei saw her as a student who needed to be saved; with Arona, the self-styled sisters living in Sensei's Shittim Chest, their joined abilities the miracle that returned the falling Sensei| [Vol F] faced fading with the Ark after the Throne of Naram-Sin's destruction; sent the alternate Chest's OS data to Arona's classroom; joined her abilities with Arona's to create the miracle ("I will join my abilities with yours to create a miracle... Do you trust me?"); [Post-Vol F] found by Arona walking alone in the void, taken home, and named Plana ("You're like a lone star in the night sky... like a planetarium"); [Vol 1 Ch 3, JP] summons Shiroko Terror to the battlefield using the power of the Chroma and, with Arona, warns that further battle risked global annihilation| The lone star named by her sister; her paper-crane seal answers Arona's whale}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: small girl, Arona's height| Skin: pale| Face: expressionless by default, faint smiles emerging [Post-Vol F]| Hair: long white hair with a red gradient, a double-helix braid, bangs over the right eye| Eyes: red-toned| Halo: a simple red ring that shifts only shades of red with her mood, versus Arona's multi-color| Outfit 1 [Default]: black sailor uniform with a long black coat; holds a black-shrouded Pattern 1914 Enfield rifle in her art, a mark of her combat-support past; paper-crane seal motif versus Arona's whale| Weapon: none personally; formerly rerouted the alternate Shittim Chest into combat support, and with Arona can invoke the Ark's residual systems when Sensei is in great crisis [Post-Vol F]}

# Nanagami Rin

SHORT_DESC: {General Student Council chief administrative officer and acting head of Kivotos throughout the President's disappearance; the only person who knew the missing President well, addressed by her as "Rinny"; the de facto administrator holding the federation together while crime rose 2000%| overworked, dutiful, publicly strict and secretly caring; analytical under pressure, near collapse from workload, quietly devoted to Kivotos' students; her professional mask cracks only around the President's memory| with the President, her lost friend, the one person allowed to call her "Rinny"; with Sensei, the outsider she recruited into SCHALE and briefs through every crisis, her trust in him growing volume by volume; with Ayane, her co-manager of the fourteen-slot Ark escape sequence, receiving "I'll leave Sensei to you, Rin!" as the slots count down| [Prologue] recruits Sensei to SCHALE, hands over the Shittim Chest, and briefs his missions; [Vol 1 onward] his constant liaison at the GSC; [Vol 4] ambushed and kidnapped by Kaya, suspended from office by the coup; [Vol F] convenes the Emergency Response Committee when six unknown masses appear over Kivotos, has her privileges frozen by the censure motion, is imprisoned inside the Schale building under Kaiser's martial law, and is rescued by the RABBIT Squad and Public Safety Bureau joint operation; joins Utnapishtim's Ship's volunteer crew [Vol F: Ark conquest] and manages the fourteen-slot escape sequence; [Post-Vol F] receives an unsigned letter addressed "To Rinny" in the President's own handwriting, confirming her friend is alive somewhere| The acting head who kept the federation alive, and the one friend the President wrote back to}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: adult-scale young woman, 164cm / 5'5", composed posture| Skin: fair| Face: oval, glasses, tired eyes behind a professional mask; bangs framing the face| Hair: long straight black hair with a dark blue inner gradient| Eyes: azure blue| Outfit 1 [Default]: dark GSC executive uniform with a white blouse, silver-rimmed glasses, three-star GSC badge shared only with the President| Weapon: white Desert Eagle Mk. XIX pistol, rarely drawn}

---

# GSC President

SHORT_DESC: {The never-named former President of the General Student Council; elected two years before the story as the youngest president ever, she ruled Kivotos nearly single-handedly through the Sanctum Tower; founded SCHALE by secret directive as a fully autonomous extrajudicial club, the last club she approved| a lover of unsolvable mysteries, the occult, and the Seven Koans, fond of Rin's version of the second koan ("By understanding the incomprehensibility of others, can we understand ourselves?"); an unpalatable sense of humor, the Eden Treaty's name is her doing; irresponsible in Yuuka's reading, "a good friend" in Rin's; her arrangements show foreknowledge of doomed futures| close to Rin, the only person who knew her well and her "Rinny"; to Sensei, the summoner who left the Shittim Chest (with Arona) waiting in the SCHALE office; to Arona and Plana, the uncanny resemblance and shared voice, a mystery in and out of universe| [Pre-Story] vanished without notice, collapsing Kivotos' administration and spiking crime 2000%; her plea video asks Sensei to "save us from this twisted, distorted fate... and find the choices that lead us to a new reality," and is interrupted in the Prologue dream by Shiroko Terror aiming a rifle at the Shittim Chest; [Vol F] appears speaking to the unconscious Sensei during the two-AI miracle ("So, let's go back, Sensei. To the place all our love exists"), deliberately ambiguous between true presence and dream; [Post-Vol F] confirmed alive somewhere through the unsigned letter in her handwriting delivered to Rin; whereabouts, condition, and the letter's contents unrevealed; she has not returned publicly through the end of Vol F| A President who prepared everything and explained nothing}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: young woman of average height, graceful bearing| Skin: fair| Face: gentle, serene, eyes often half-lidded; bangs over the left eye| Hair: long sky-blue hair with a light pink inner side, a loose braid on the left| Eyes: soft blue| Halo: white ring with a blue star center, mirroring the Blue Archive logo| Outfit 1 [Pre-Story]: white jacket over a double-breasted dress with gold buttons, three-star GSC badge; presidential regalia in her plea video| Weapon: none; her authority was the Sanctum Tower itself}

---

# Takanashi Hoshino

SHORT_DESC: {President of the Abydos Student Council and chairwoman of the Foreclosure Task Force, the only registered 3rd-year at Abydos High School; praised as a genius and a Super-Elite in her first year, once active, fierce, and belligerent, now the academy's resident "auntie"| speaks like a senile aunt, enjoys playing around and napping more than work, and gets scolded by the rest of the Committee for it; once a mission starts she becomes the front line itself, fighting valiantly with shotgun and shield to protect her comrades; since accepting the title of student council president she has gradually brightened up, perhaps this being the real Hoshino| [Pre-Story] her younger self, vice president of the old student council under Yume: guarded by a traumatic past, extremely serious, stern, unapproachable, straightforward, blunt, grumpy, with zero tolerance for slackers or nonsense; fiercely independent with an ironclad stubborn will, refusing to rely on others or show even a fraction of vulnerability in the belief that exposed weakness invites destruction; a rigid militaristic mindset, cynical and cold, fully willing to resort to overwhelming tactical violence, making both allies and enemies deeply wary of her presence| the Committee's inverse hierarchy in full swing: the chairwoman lazes while Shiroko scolds her, Serika nags, Ayane fusses, and Nonomi smiles through it, all absorbed with languid grace; Yume's death is the debt she can never pay, the grief the auntie persona is built over, and the reason she stayed in the dying district as its last senior; toward Sensei the guarded senior slowly learning to be rescued, after he talked the Black Suit down for her sake| [Vol 1] the volume's deuteragonist, she privately accepts the Black Suit's offer and "enlists" in Kaiser PMC in exchange for the debt's forgiveness, actually delivered into Gematria's experiments, defects when Abydos comes under attack, is rescued by the Task Force, and is fought over by the Black Suit in the "Battle Between Adults"; [Vol F] spearheads the First Sanctum assault in the Abydos Desert, wonders at the masked Shiroko's motives during the Ark conquest, and receives her rescued friend back; [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP] the Underground Dweller re-triggers her Yume trauma and transforms her into the awakened god-entity "Horus of Dawn," from which she is rescued by Sensei's fifth Adult Card use| The Black Suit rates her mystic the greatest in Kivotos; sleepy outside, unkillable inside}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 145cm / 4'9", small-framed and deceptively slight, sleepy posture; carries her shield like paper| Skin: fair| Face: round and youthful beneath heavy sleep-lidded eyes, a small cute fang on the right visible when she smiles; the languid auntie expression drawn over the frame of a former Super-Elite| Hair: pink, reaching her legs in the back with a cowlick in front, worn loose with a small braid and her signature whale hairpins; [Pre-Story: Young] a short bob framing the face, low twin-tails near the ears on simple black hair ties, a prominent twitching ahoge on top| Eyes: heterochromatic, bright sky-blue left and golden-yellow right; [Pre-Story: Young] the same colors but a cold, piercing gaze, devoid of the usual sleepy warmth| Halo: pink, an eye-like circle with an outer line curved around and ending in a strip| Outfit 1 [Default]: the Abydos uniform worn relaxed, blazer omitted, loose white collared dress shirt with top buttons undone and cyan-blue tie loosely fastened, student ID badge clipped at the left chest, black tactical shoulder harness with matching waist belt, dark gray pleated plaid skirt with a subtle grid, fingerless black tactical gloves, mismatched white socks (standard ribbed left, loose ankle right), navy high-top sneakers| Outfit 2 [Pre-Story: Young]: a crisp, strictly fastened uniform under a black tactical bulletproof vest with utility pockets and chest pistol holster, red-and-white striped insignia tag, Abydos armband on the right upper arm, symmetric white knee-high socks, black tactical boots| Outfit 3 [Gym]: white top with light blue trim, collar pulled to one side, black athletic shorts, white hand cloth on the right hand| Weapon: "Eye of Horus", a two-tone Beretta 1301 Tactical semi-automatic shotgun, vent-ribbed 21-inch Comp barrel with a +3 extension tube, white with pink accents, Abydos insignia on the cheek rest; deploys the "Iron Horus" collapsible ballistic shield housing spare buckshot rounds and a backup Beretta 92FS}

# Sunaookami Shiroko

SHORT_DESC: {Abydos Foreclosure Task Force field captain, 2nd-year, the quiet rationalist obsessed with money-making schemes to save the school; a girl of few words and almost no facial expression who reads as frigid and emotionless, in truth more devoted to Abydos High School than anything else, willing to do what it takes for its revitalization and sometimes proposing the outlandish ideas herself| soft-spoken and deeply caring beneath the calm; hyperfocuses on her interests, oblivious to how others react to them; friendly and relaxed around the committee yet vindictive and aggressive when provoked, occasionally acting on instinct with a surprisingly energetic side; thinks distances of tens of kilometers are average, fantasizes about bank robberies and survival fishing trips, and pouts with remarkable petulance when denied either| the inverse hierarchy's scolder, the junior who chides chairwoman Hoshino's napping while treasuring the striped scarf the young Hoshino gave her [Pre-Story]; ready to fight the Gehenna head prefect Hina on sight, stopping only at Ayane's begging; needs Sensei and Ayane to hold her back and keep her sensible; filled Wakamo with lead in jealousy over her clinginess toward Sensei; rescued the lost Sensei in the desert on his first day| [Vol 1] rescues Sensei, proposes and leads the masked swimsuit-gang heist on the Stygian Bank that exposes Kaiser as the true creditor; [Vol F] tails Kaiser PMC's suspicious movements in the Abydos Desert, loses contact after a mysterious encounter with her other-timeline self, and is taken by the Chroma, held captive inside the Ark of Atrahasis; rescued by Sensei in the Central District; [Vol F: finale] gives her bank-robbery balaclava to the other Shiroko as a keepsake ("You could rob any bank with this") and proposes they go cycling together someday; [Post-Vol F] cycles again with the Task Force, her bicycling habit restored, quietly living beside the second Shiroko who shares her name; [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP] steps in Hoshino's way when the president tries to handle the Nonomi rescue alone, is defeated by her dominant display of skill, and rises again with the committee to stop her| Nicknames her schemes "plans"; speaks in flat declaratives; her greatest fear is losing the everyday life of the committee}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 156cm / 5'1", lean, athletic, strong cycling legs| Skin: fair| Face: calm, minimal expression, wolf-like cool gaze| Hair: grey shoulder-length bob; [Pre-Story: Young] short, nape-length, hairpin and wolf ears unchanged| Eyes: light blue with mismatched slitted pupils, the left one white| Halo: cyan ring with four cardinal lines and an inner blue circle, a gun crosshair| Ears: grey wolf ears, white fluff, black insides; a cyan inverted cross hairpin below the left ear; humanoid ears beneath the hair, a cyan stud at the right| Outfit 1 [Default]: standard Abydos uniform, tucked white dress shirt, striped cyan scarf, cyan tie with a navy cross mark, silver-buttoned navy blazer, lime-green and cyan Abydos badge at the left, plaid dark-grey skirt, fingerless cycling glove, black running shoes with cyan laces; a shoulder bag with three keychains, a winking green cat, a silver ankh, a pink heart| Outfit 2 [Vol 1: bank raid]: blue number-two mask with wolf ears exposed, kept in her schoolbag between robberies, worn with the committee's swimsuits for the heist| Outfit 3 [Pre-Story: Young]: navy blazer buttoned over a white shirt, blue tie, the blue-and-black striped scarf a gift from young Hoshino, a large gun-carrying backpack| Weapon: "White Fang 465", a white SIG SG 550 with an M4 lower receiver, black suppressor and vertical grip, cyan charging handle and selector, SUSAT L9A1 sight with blue lens, Abydos emblem on the mag well, "ABYDOS 550" on the suppressor; a white missile drone, black and cyan accents, twin rotors, missile pods}

# Sunaookami Shiroko Terror (Shiroko＊Terror / Anubis)

SHORT_DESC: {The Sunaookami Shiroko of a destroyed parallel timeline, formally titled "God of Death, Anubis," a Nameless Goddess and the first member of the Iridescent Vanguard; the game's only character from a different timeline of Kivotos, the same being as this world's Shiroko though a little more grown up| [Pre-Story: her timeline] in her world Schale and Abydos fell, her friends and her Sensei were lost, and she alone remained; ordered by the Nameless Priests to shoot the injured alternate Sensei, she could not pull the trigger; despairing, she made contact with the Chroma and was reversed into a Terror, her mystic inverted into the god of death who "guides the living to the otherworld"; came into this world to fulfill the role of bringing it to its predetermined end, yet remains hesitant after coming to know certain people; insists the fall is her own will ("This is me. This is my will... the Chroma is under mine") and that the future is predetermined ("This world's fate was sealed when Sunaookami Shiroko came to exist within it"); deeply cares for Abydos and will do whatever it takes to rebuild the school and the region, sometimes showing an eccentric side| kept her distance from her other self in the belief that two Shirokos in one world is not allowed, until the mask gift and "It should be okay to have two Shirokos here"; breaks down at the sight of the living Foreclosure Task Force, her dead friends alive and whole; saved by Sensei, who forfeits his own last escape slot for her; her identity known only to the Task Force and Hina, while the public believes she died on the Ark| [Vol F: Prologue] appears in Sensei's dream aiming a rifle at the Shittim Chest; [Ch 1] the "mysterious girl" whose desert encounter takes our Shiroko, then teleports into the Gematria meeting and destroys the organization; [Ch 2-3] Ark-side antagonist guarding the Dimension Engines, warning Sensei to leave or be shot; [Ch 4] fights beside Phrenapates in the final battle and is defeated; [Post-Vol F] healed, wearing her light-blue halo again; accepted our Shiroko's bank-robbery balaclava as a keepsake; chose to remain in this world, living in the Abydos district as a scavenging watchful guardian repairing and selling items abandoned to the desertification; listed on the Abydos Student Council; takes up cycling again despite initial reluctance; [Vol 1 Ch 3, JP] summoned by Plana to fight the transformed Hoshino, severs our Shiroko's contact with the Chroma, and terrifies the Underground Dweller into permanent exile| Two Shirokos coexist ("Sensei will figure it out")}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 165cm / 5'5", grown from Shiroko's 156, athletic and harder-edged| Skin: pale| Face: unmistakably Shiroko's and yet not quite the same, a colder and wearier set| Hair: misty-grey, waist-length, wolf ears sharing her hair color; a cyan T-shaped hairpin near the left temple| Eyes: cyan with heterochromia, a white pupil on the left and a black one on the right, both vertical slits| Halo: [Vol F] black with a slight dark-green glow around the edges and shattered pieces on one section; [Post-Vol F] turned light blue, healed in part through Sensei's guidance| Ears: wolf ears with white fluff inside| Outfit 1 [Vol F: Anubis]: black bustier dress parting at the left thigh, long-sleeved with white cuffs and a dark blue-green interior, black choker, holster on the left thigh, dark grey strapped high heels| Outfit 2 [Post-Vol F: keepsake]: our Shiroko's blue number-two bank-robbery balaclava, "You could rob any bank with this"| Weapon: "Black Fang 465", her counterpart's SIG SG 550 repainted mostly black, marks of repair on a well-maintained rifle, the Abydos logo and "Abydos 550" barely visible; a SIG Sauer P226R in cutscenes, most notably aimed at the Shittim Chest; as the Vol F boss re-enacts the entire Foreclosure Task Force arsenal, her missile drone, Ayane's helicopter and healing drone, Hoshino's shield and shotgun, Nonomi's rotary machine gun, and Serika's weapons, the playable version keeping only her drone; her EX skill burns 20% of her own max HP to summon it and prevents retreat below 1% HP}

# Kuromi Serika

SHORT_DESC: {Abydos Foreclosure Task Force treasurer, 1st-year, the committee's tsundere workhorse juggling a parade of part-time jobs, from the Shibaseki Ramen counter to convenience stores, to keep pace with the debt's interest, her wages going straight into Abydos High's payments| has difficulty expressing her true emotions; often short-tempered and angry, in reality kind and hardworking; scolds everyone's slacking while quietly keeping the committee solvent; hot-tempered, loud, secretly softhearted| invites Sensei to work her multiple part-time jobs alongside her, the closest thing she has to admitting she wants him around; her tirade that "grown-ups can't be trusted" marks Sensei's first wall at Abydos; the shared meal the committee trails her to at Shibaseki Ramen ends with her still sullen, her gratitude arriving sideways; nags chairwoman Hoshino's lazing alongside Shiroko; with Ayane, her steady partner, visiting the hospitalized Master Shiba together, digging through administrative documents, and beginning the investigation of Yume's death| [Vol 1] trailed to her ramen-shop shift by Sensei and the committee, kidnapped by the Helmet Gang on her way back from work, and rescued when Sensei uses Schale's authority to triangulate her phone; her workplace's building is seized by the Kaiser Corporation in the same war; burns out from overwork mid-volume and is saved by her friends; [Vol F] fights in the vanguard of the First Sanctum assault in the Abydos Desert with Problem Solver 68; [Post-Vol F] continues her jobs with the committee intact, and with Ayane digs into the old student council's records| Voice of the ordinary student's exhaustion and the reason the debt feels personal}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 153cm / 5'0", small, wiry from constant labor| Skin: fair| Face: sharp-eyed, quick to scowl, blushes easily when thanked; a canine tooth, a cute little fang| Hair: navy blue, twintails reaching to her legs, tied with bright cyan ribbons| Eyes: reddish-pink with long vertical pink pupils| Halo: a red crosshair with arrows on the outer ring| Ears: dark blue cat ears; a speaker earpiece worn at one ear| Outfit 1 [Default]: formal Abydos uniform, black blazer, white shirt, blue necktie, black pleated skirt; unique to her, an ID card with red markings and a black kneeguard on the right knee| Outfit 2 [Work: Shibaseki Ramen]: black shirt and blue bow tie, employee ID badge at the left of her shirt, blue apron with the restaurant's logo, a holster with a pistol at her right side, a staff headscarf| Outfit 3 [Gym]: black sports jacket with blue pockets, white sleeves and black trim, blue hem and collar, the Abydos logo at the left side, a black watch with a red bezel, black-and-white shoes with blue laces, white sole and cyan center; owns a dark pink balaclava bearing the number four for the committee's bank raids| Weapon: "Sincerity", a two-tone Beretta AR-70/223 assault rifle, dark blue flash hider, gas block, stock, handguard and pistol grip over a white receiver, barrel and gas tube, a Vortex Crossfire II riflescope with a red lens, the Abydos insignia engraved on the stock and the school's name imprinted on the receiver above the grip}

# Izayoi Nonomi

SHORT_DESC: {Abydos Foreclosure Task Force member, 2nd-year, the cheerful heiress and emotional support of the committee; born into a very rich family that stayed loyal to Abydos when other families fled the desertification, she enrolled by choice where her fortune could matter most, though she has never said so herself| emotional and gentle, serving as mental support for a committee of extreme personalities; friendly, pleasant, endlessly encouraging; most of the committee's snacks come out of her allowance| the committee's frontline muscle whose morale keeps Serika upright and whose suppressing fire covers everyone else; her butler stands one call away for intelligence, explaining the Nephthys dilemma when the creditors come; with Hoshino, the senior who came for her when the creditors would not; with Suou and Nephthys, the creditors who took her hostage and offered her a way out she declined| [Vol 1] provides the muscle of the committee's frontline with her rotary machine gun, the masked swimsuit-gang heist of the Stygian Bank included, her green number-three robber hat and all; [Vol F] carries the suppressing fire of the First Sanctum assault in the Abydos Desert vanguard; [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP] taken hostage in Nephthys's contract gambit, announces she will drop out of Abydos to settle it, is rescued by Hoshino and the students, and declines the offer to re-enroll at Highlander Railroad Academy, choosing Abydos for good as the district fills with vitality again| She could pay the debt herself; the Task Force refuses to let one person carry it, and she accepts this}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 160cm / 5'3", softly built| Skin: fair| Face: warm oval face, a default gentle smile| Hair: pale blonde, long to her waist, a side bun on the left| Eyes: emerald green| Halo: neon green circle with a smaller circle inside and two earlaps| Outfit 1 [Default]: Abydos uniform forgoing tie and blazer, white shirt under a beige cardigan with black piping worn loosely off her shoulders, Abydos access badge on a lanyard, grey skirt, black socks, white trainers, black tracking gloves| Outfit 2 [Vol 1: bank raid]: a green hooded bank robber's hat with the number three, worn with the committee's swimsuits for the Stygian Bank heist| Outfit 3 [Pre-Story: Young]: neat formal uniform, dark navy blazer with thin light-blue piping along the lapels and pockets over a black vest, white collared dress shirt, vibrant cyan-blue tie, dark gray pleated plaid skirt, solid black tights, gray loafers, a large heavy black equipment bag slung over her shoulder, presumably containing her minigun| Weapon: "Little Machine Gun V", a black and white M134 Minigun with hints of green, a chainsaw grip attached above the receiver for hip-fire portability, a box magazine attached below, the Abydos insignia on the housing}

# Okusora Ayane

SHORT_DESC: {Abydos Foreclosure Task Force secretary, 1st-year, the committee's intelligence and logistics anchor; a serious secretary of the Countermeasures Committee, the type who values principles and rules, doing her earnest to restore Abydos High| shy and patient, displaying great tolerance for her committee members' often wacky ideas and plans; once her patience is exhausted she has been known to throw a tantrum of epic proportions; keeps the paperwork, ledgers, and schedules alive with long-suffering politeness| the brakes of the committee, the one whose begging stops Shiroko's fight with the Gehenna head prefect Hina, and who with Sensei keeps Shiroko sensible; with Serika, her partner in visiting the hospitalized Master Shiba, digging through administrative documents, and investigating Yume's death; with Sensei, the girl he trusts with the acting presidency; with Rin, her co-manager of the Ark escape, telling her "I'll leave Sensei to you, Rin!" as the fourteen slots count down| [Vol 1] finds signs of contraband in the Helmet Gang's tank wreckage, digs out the administrative documents proving Kaiser acquired most of the district's land, and authors the closing letter thanking Sensei for preserving the committee's way of life; guides the rescue that triangulates Serika's phone; [Vol F] serves in the First Sanctum vanguard and in the Ark escape management, tells Sensei with Rin that Phrenapates must be defeated with Utnapishtim's main weapon, and refuses to leave the falling Ark before Sensei and Shiroko are rescued ("I'll stay with you until the end!"); [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP] appointed acting Student Council president by Sensei during the Nephthys crisis, overturning Hoshino's self-sacrificial orders| Red glasses catch the light when she lectures; her diligence is the committee's true spine}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 153cm / 5'0", small, bookish posture| Skin: fair| Face: round gentle face behind red glasses, an apologetic default expression| Hair: black bob cut with bangs, a French-braid styled headband matching her hair color, a white butterfly hairpin with a red stud| Eyes: amber| Halo: a red crosshair inside another circle, with holes on the oval part of the crosshair| Ears: elven, pointed| Outfit 1 [Default]: standard Abydos uniform, navy blue blazer with the Abydos access badge and cuffed sleeves, beige sweater over a tucked-in white shirt and cyan tie, pleated plaid black skirt, a cyan armband with dark stripes, black socks with a butterfly on the rim, brown uwabaki school slippers with red laces, a speaker earpiece, a large satchel of documents| Outfit 2 [Vol 1: bank raid]: a yellow bank robber mask with the number zero, an equipment item rarely seen| Outfit 3 [Gym]: gym shirt with blue cuffs and hem under a gym jacket like Serika's, calf-length white socks, black-and-white sneakers with a cyan midsole| Weapon: "Common Sense", a white SIG Sauer P229 pistol with a black grip and red trigger, a red tassel with a white butterfly accessory below the grip, the Abydos insignia on the left side of the slide; deploys a black quadrotor Health Kit Drone that drops medic kits on the field, remotely controlled through her laptop}

# Kuchinashi Yume

SHORT_DESC: {Former president of the Abydos Student Council and founding member of the Foreclosure Task Force, deceased before the main story; the final president of Abydos High School's old student council; a kind, selfless, slightly clumsy big-sister figure with cyan hair and band-aids| believed and relied on miracles a bit too much, in her junior Hoshino's reading naive and "a fool"; her competence short of her title, approaching major problems recklessly without awareness of the consequences; blinded by her own hope, her optimism and excessive kindness shading into toxic positivity, making reckless decisions and continuing on despite how pointless the efforts were; forgetful; naive enough to let others take advantage of her; against violence, believing it a poor solution that would only worsen; through everything, a smile as bright as her hope always on her face| Hoshino's senior and the loss that defines her, the two co-founding the committee and treasure-hunting the desert together in the old days; her last quarrel with Hoshino, over a festival poster Hoshino tore up, fell on the day she vanished; the contract bearing her signature surfaces years later to haunt the committee; the only student in Kivotos never to have met Sensei in the current timeline| [Pre-Story] she held the old Abydos together as the district collapsed around the school and died during the old Abydos crisis; [Vol 1] her existence surfaces through Hoshino's guarded memories; [Vol 2-F] her death remains the defining trauma beneath Hoshino's persona and the reason the Task Force refuses to let Hoshino sacrifice herself again; [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP] the full circumstances surface, she was stranded in the Abydos desert without compass or water and died of exposure and dehydration, keeping her barely-functioning broken phone because replacing it would cost money needed for the debt; the blue mineral treasure she and Hoshino had searched the desert for burns at the oasis as the arc closes; an alternate-timeline Yume who knew Sensei for quite a while meets him in "Apology," knowing they are off to rescue a suffering child| The only student confirmed dead in the current timeline before Phrenapates's death}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: teenage girl of unlisted height, light frame| Skin: fair| Face: soft cheerful face, a smile as bright as her hope; band-aids scattered on her arms and legs| Hair: cyan, knee-length, loose with a long cowlick reaching her neck| Eyes: golden yellow| Halo: two yellow rings with the Abydos symbol in a diamond within them, the only student halo resembling her school's emblem| Outfit 1 [Pre-Story: Default]: tucked-in white shirt with a cyan necktie tucked into her strap, tactical belts on both shoulders and around the waist, black plaid skirt, white socks, black sneakers with white laces and soles; Hoshino's current relaxed uniform is generally believed to be based on hers| Outfit 2 [Swimsuit variant]: dark navy one-piece school swimsuit with the Abydos logo at the right chest, three armbands on the right arm and two on the left, bandages on both legs, dark blue flip-flops| Weapon: a grey Beretta 90two pistol with a silver barrel and black grips; formerly owned the collapsible ballistic shield "Iron Horus" that could hold the pistol and spare magazines, both kept for safekeeping by Hoshino and used in her armed variant}

# Sorasaki Hina

SHORT_DESC: {Chairwoman of Gehenna Academy's Prefect Team, 3rd-year, 17, the feared face of the academy's public order; feared by every student in Gehenna, if not all of Kivotos, and rumored to hold most of the strength of the academy's entirety; a workaholic secretly considerate, she works to reform the lawless academy from the inside| at heart a troublesome, laid-back person who calls school rules a "hassle"; at the academy she becomes strict, enacting calm and quick judgment without hesitation on the battlefield, where her fighting prowess is astounding and any organization that opposes Gehenna fears her more than anyone else from the Prefect Team; cold and distant on the outside, she is relaxed and soft when allowed to express herself, always busy with work she finds bothersome and exhausting; [Vol 3] should the Eden Treaty be signed she wished to retire, exhausted by everything involving Gehenna; found spiraling after everything went wrong, she broke down believing herself less mentally strong than Hoshino, revealing that she too wanted to be praised, comforted, and acknowledged by Sensei just like the other students, and pulled herself together after his genuine "thank you"| Ako is always at her side as devoted secretary, Iori leads her assault corps, and her subordinates idolize and quietly fear her; Makoto of the Pandemonium Society bullies her and her Disciplinary Committee for petty reasons; battles Hoshino fiercely aboard the train from Junction Station toward the Great Oasis and, with Hoshino fallen, tells her the truth about Yume; toward Sensei the exhausted chairwoman slowly learning to be thanked| [Vol 1] when the Prefect Team's shelling interrupts the Problem Solver 68 vs Foreclosure Task Force battle, she arrives, hears Ako's report, promptly apologizes for the Team overstepping into Abydos territory, and pulls Gehenna's forces out while slipping Sensei the hint that Kaiser Corporation is up to something in the Abydos Desert; later brings the Prefect Team to the Task Force's defense against Kaiser PMC at Sensei's request and destroys the Train Cannon with Makoto; [Vol 3] handles the Gourmet Research Society's new recruits alongside Sena, shares her view of the Eden Treaty with Sensei, battles the resurrected Saint Justina Council to protect him, shields him from Saori's attack in the Day of Fire and Ash and is wounded, vanishes while recovering, comes out of her brief retirement after a visit from Sensei, and rejoins the treaty's defense; [Vol F] joins the Third Sanctum assault at the abandoned amusement park in Slumpia with the RABBIT Squad and Game Development Department against Shiro & Kuro, the only Prefect absent from Gehenna's district defense because she is on the offensive| Knee-length white hair with glowing-cracked horns; the smallest chairwoman Gehenna has had}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 142cm / 4'8", small, upright military posture belying her size| Skin: fair| Face: composed, faintly tired eyes; a golden clip with an attached monocle adorns her bangs; [Vol 3: Nightwear] exhaustion evident, dark bags beneath the eyes| Hair: knee-length wavy white tied with a black-and-red ribbon; outstretched dark black horns with glowing purple cracks, the glow gone when exhausted| Eyes: purple, a devil's gaze| Halo: a black jagged cylinder with purple stripes resembling a hole saw, a black circle within and a purple ring inside that bearing a sharp arrow pointing down, two black arrows in the outer ring; appears solid like a demon's crown where most students' halos look hollow and ethereal| Wings: long black bat wings from the back of her waist, limp when exhausted| Outfit 1 [Default]: traditional military uniform, dark purple coat embroidered with golden metals and buttons, red military armband on the unworn sleeve of a black furry cloak with a white interior, two badges at the left hip plus a smaller black-and-yellow one beneath, short black pencil skirt, wine-purple thigh-high stockings, dark purple military boots with golden latches| Outfit 2 [Vol 3: Nightwear]: cream-colored cardigan over a pink slip dress with a white polka-dot pattern, gray slippers, all accessories removed and hair let down| Weapon: "The End: Destroyer", a black MG42 machine gun with a dark violet pistol grip, custom thumb-hole stock, a lever that pops open the top cover, and a barrel nozzle with spiral cuts emitting purple light}

# Shiromi Iori

SHORT_DESC: {Field captain of the Gehenna Prefect Team's assault corps, 2nd-year, 16, a cold-blooded enforcement specialist who immediately punishes students violating the rules with overwhelming force wherever she finds them; efficient and formidable in combat, reckless and stubborn, prone to losing sight of her surroundings once she spots an enemy, which makes it easy for her to fall for even obvious traps| strict with the rules to the point of punishing any and all rule-breakers; her patrol schedule never pauses for the holidays, and she is quite exhausted from dealing with troublemakers even then; her lectures on conduct are as feared as her rifle; respected even by the delinquents she hunts, feared by the students she patrols| serves Hina's Prefect Team as its field blade, with Chinatsu, the junior prefect, drilling herself toward Iori's standards and Ako smoothing the paperwork her enforcement leaves behind; toward Sensei a formidable opponent turned warily valued ally after he out-commands her assault corps in Abydos| [Vol 1] her assault corps shells the Problem Solver 68 vs Foreclosure Task Force battlefield and declares the arrest of PS68 on the spot, claiming Gehenna's authority, which the Task Force rebuts as void on Abydos territory; defeated alongside her reinforcements through Sensei's command until Ako arrives to mediate; [Vol 3] her team intercepts the Make-Up Work Club arriving in Gehenna for the relocated second exam, mistaking them for the Justice Task Force, until the Gourmet Research Society clears the way; [Vol F] fronts Gehenna's district defense with every available prefect while Hina fights at the Third Sanctum, holding the line against the False Sanctums' guardians| Her marksmanship never needs a second shot; her self-training regimen never takes a day off}

# Amau Ako

SHORT_DESC: {Senior administrator of the Gehenna Prefect Team and secretary to the head prefect, 3rd-year, 17, Hina's devoted secretary and the unit's diplomatic brain, always at the chairwoman's side assisting her; at first glance kind and good-natured, she is composed in public and fawning in private, handling the paperwork, apologies, and negotiations Hina is too blunt for| the Team's open secret is her devotion; she files Hina's overtime reports with personal annotations and treats the chairwoman's exhaustion as a personal emergency, her official hobby listed as Hina herself; toward the Foreclosure Task Force a polite, fruitless negotiating partner; toward Sensei the reasonable face of Gehenna whenever the Prefect Team oversteps, and the first to worry when Hina's workload breaks her| [Vol 1] steps in to mediate after the Prefect Team's failed arrest of Problem Solver 68 and the Foreclosure Task Force's victory over her reinforcements; her talks with the Task Force remain fruitless, leaving the standoff hostile until Hina herself arrives, hears Ako's report, and apologizes; [Vol 3 onward] continues administering the Team through the treaty crisis and the Chroma war, including the chairwoman's wounded convalescence and brief retirement; [Vol F] arrives by vehicle at Hina's side in D.U. as the tower falls and the calamity begins, then stands with every Prefect at Gehenna's district defense under Iori while Hina fights at the Third Sanctum, coordinating the defense's logistics behind the front line| Every letter Gehenna sends abroad passes through her ledger; every apology is handwritten}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 165cm / 5'5", tall and willowy, a secretary's posture| Skin: fair| Face: an elegant, polite smile with calculating eyes| Hair: dark purple-blue, long, straight| Eyes: pink| Halo: a soft violet ring| Outfit 1 [Default]: the Gehenna prefect uniform in the secretary variant, long skirt, ledger bag| Weapon: "Hot Shot", a handgun kept close for the chairwoman's sake}

# Hinomiya Chinatsu

SHORT_DESC: {Prefect Team member, 1st-year, 15, in charge of medical aid at Gehenna Academy's Prefect Team and one of the few people with common sense among the obsessively dogmatic Prefect Team, usually the one reining in its more headlong members; a perfectionist junior prefect drilling herself toward Iori's standards; earnest, easily flustered when praised, meticulous in patrol reports that read like after-action analyses; her hobby is reading philosophy books| looks up to Iori's field discipline and measures herself against it; Hina is the distant standard of order she trains toward; crosses paths with the Emergency Medicine Club's Sena on the casualty lines without rivalry; toward Sensei the junior who takes his praise with a flustered bow| [Vol 1 onward] patrols Gehenna's streets during the PS68 manhunts and the post-treaty instability, her medical bag always packed; [Vol 3] on the patrol lines as the Prefect Team intercepts the Make-Up Work Club arriving for the relocated second exam and the Gourmet Research Society clears the way; [Vol F] stands with the Prefect Team's district defense under Iori as every member but Hina holds the line against the False Sanctums' guardians, learning to hold a fixed line| Keeps a personal notebook of every delinquent case she fails to close; believes Gehenna can be orderly someday}

# Hanuma Makoto

SHORT_DESC: {Chairwoman of the Pandemonium Society, Gehenna's student council and thus the academy's president, 3rd-year, 18; an ambitious leader whose schemes know no bounds, an eccentric hyperactive schemer whose grand initiatives drive Gehenna's chaotic politics, launched with total conviction and abandoned just as fast| an immature and dimwitted tyrant who puts her own passions first; her thoughtless plans often get the Society into trouble, which she shrugs off as if none of it was her fault; a strong-willed girl who quickly recovers from any failure; deep down she holds a very strong respect for everybody's free will, and having lived under the old Thunder Emperor's tyranny she carries an unpleasant feeling toward the Disciplinary Committee, whose suppression of chaos throughout Gehenna Academy reminds her of the old memory; her listed hobbies are plotting, conspiring, and playing with Ibuki; treats coups, budgets, and treaties as equally fun games| loves Ibuki like a younger sister and treats Iroha like one as well; likes to bully Hina and her Disciplinary Committee for petty reasons; toward Sensei the president who treats his visits as the best game of the week| [Vol 1] with Hina destroys the Train Cannon, the inheritance of the Tyrant Emperor, as Abydos is freed; [Vol 3] receives Sensei before the Eden Treaty signing and agrees to meet again at the ceremony, committing the Society to Gehenna's side of the treaty; during the Day of Fire and Ash the Arius Squad's missiles strike the Pandemonium Society and Prefect Team as they set forth from Gehenna, the Society's leadership ends up hospitalized, and order returns with their recovery; [Vol F] the Society's presence at Gehenna's district defense amounts to Satsuki alone showing up, unhelpfully; the Society proper saves the Ninjutsu Research Club on the Hyakkiyako snowfield, arriving from the skies as the promised salvation before withdrawing| Her laugh echoes through three corridors}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 169cm / 5'7", tall, restless energy, never still| Skin: fair| Face: a wide grin, glittering scheming eyes; bangs covering the right eye| Hair: really long, messy white hair reaching her feet, strands framing the face, voluminous at the ends| Eyes: blue| Horns: four symmetrical horns rising from her head, arching backwards with a curve before tapering to sharp points| Halo: a plus shape with sharp edges and an asymmetric three-quarter circle, red going to blood black at the edges| Outfit 1 [Default]: a military-style uniform beneath a long and baggy black-and-red military coat with gold accents, epaulettes, and red sleeves, a white shirt with black tie underneath, a black skirt decorated with golden buttons and two red lines, black sheer hosiery, black boots, and a matching military hat adorned with an emblem of a bandage with bat-like wings| Weapon: "Solitude's Edge", a gold Walther WA 2000 sniper rifle with black handguard and cheek rest, outfitted with a Schmidt & Bender 2.5-10x riflescope; commands a battery of 8.8cm Flak 18 guns}

# Rikuhachima Aru

SHORT_DESC: {Self-proclaimed "Boss" of Problem Solver 68, Gehenna's 2nd-year jack-of-all-trades mercenary club, conducting her semi-legal business of errands, enforcers, and odd jobs however she pleases; her true goal is making Problem Solver 68 a reputable criminal organization| puts on a cool, beauteous, villainous facade but breaks character constantly under teasing or pressure, exposing a meticulous and caring leader; behind the mischievous persona a shy artist striving for perfection; thirsty for knowledge of the criminal underworld she romanticizes, idolizing the image of a criminal without comprehending its true nature; determined and relentless until a task is thoroughly accomplished, yet goofy and prone to panicking when plans go sideways; competent enough, with focus making up for talent| the club she leads and mothers: Kayoko's deadpan muscle, Mutsuki's pranks that crack her facade on schedule, Haruka's anxious devotion, all quietly hers to care for; constantly tries to impress Sensei and repeatedly invites him to join Problem Solver 68, repeatedly refused, repeatedly undeterred| [Vol 1] hired by Kaiser PMC through the Helmet Gang chain to attack Abydos High School; treated to ramen by the very students she was hired against, her guilty conscience collapses at Master Shiba's warm welcome, Haruka's panic-bombing of the shop completes the disaster, and she plays the villain the Task Force believes her to be, clashing with them until Kaiser's cannons interrupt; after the FTF spares the stolen money for documents, PS68 pockets the leftover bundles; returns at the Abydos siege "in the nick of time" to break the Kaiser board member's blockade and help recover Hoshino; [Vol F] serves in the vanguard of the First Sanctum assault beside the Foreclosure Task Force in the Abydos desert, her mercenary pride finally on the right ledger| Her horn rims glow when she poses; Kayoko keeps the receipts}

# Asagi Mutsuki

# Kurodate Haruna

SHORT_DESC: {President of the Gourmet Research Society, Gehenna, 3rd-year, 17, an elegant endlessly hungry gourmet; at a glance she possesses the graceful charisma of a refined lady, but when it comes to food she can turn into a deranged maniac at the flip of a switch; composed speech, a scholar's method, a tyrant's appetite, zero tolerance for mediocrity, dining with full ceremony even in foxholes; her listed hobbies are foodie touring and making foodie lists| the Society's members, Junko, Izumi, and Akari, are her retinue of fellow appetites, swept along by her campaigns and judged by her standards; Fuuka of the School Lunch Club is the cook she respects and drags along when the food is historic, praising her excellent work in the Ark's lowest level as Izumi carries the overwhelmed girl out; Hina and Sena handled her Society's new recruits after the Trinity disturbance, the Prefect Team's wary truce with the appetite; toward Sensei the host who assumes adults appreciate a full course| [Vol 3] her Society's midnight rampage through a Trinity dessert shop nearly reignites the Trinity-Gehenna conflict on the eve of the Eden Treaty, forcing Hasumi, the Make-Up Work Club, and Sensei to intervene as the members flee with the Justice Task Force in pursuit; later the Society clears the way for the Make-Up Work Club's relocated exam at Gehenna, and is handled by Hina and Sena alongside its new recruits; [Vol F] deployed to defend D.U. Shiratori City against the Sixth Sanctum, learns the Kaitengers are active in the area, joins Hifumi and Azusa in facing the guardian Perorodzilla, then slips away from the pursuers with Fuuka into the lowest level of the Ark of Atrahasis, where the Society destroys the temporary object the enemy's plan needed, beginning the failure cascade that readies the Ark's self-destruction, and escapes declaring a full course meal is over only after dessert| Her chopsticks are antiques; her lists are the Society's scripture}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 163cm / 5'4", graceful, the posture of a tea ceremony host| Skin: fair| Face: a composed oval face, a knowing half-smile, eyes closed in contentment when eating| Hair: long straight black hair with blunt bangs| Eyes: red| Halo: a pale crimson ring with a lotus motif| Outfit 1 [Default]: the Gehenna uniform worn in traditional style, a haori coat, a lacquered lunch box at the hip| Weapon: "Ideal", a sniper rifle carried with tea-ceremony calm}

SHORT_DESC: {One of the three presidents of the Tea Party, Trinity General School's student council, 3rd-year, leader of the Filius Faction, and the calculating main antagonist of Volume 3's first half; the Tea Party president with the highest political prowess, a rigid guardian of Trinity's unity who thinks first and overthinks second, to the point of causing trouble for people who are uninvolved or supposed to be considered innocent| calm and composed with tea-ceremony stillness, yet quite ruthless the instant someone breaks her composure; treats the Make-Up Work Club's students as variables in her trap until the apology she owes all of them; interrogates Sensei over his meeting with Mika and is refused, Sensei declaring he will block her flurry of doubt and see the entire club pass; hosts Mika's expulsion hearing with Sakurako and Mine at her side, the pair joining her "in the name of helping the Tea Party"| [Vol 3] proposes that Sensei take charge of the Make-Up Work Club, revealing to him privately that the club's true function is to guide failing students toward expulsion, all part of her plan to expose Trinity's traitor; expands the second exam's scope and passing bar overnight, relocates the test site to Gehenna, and her interference fails the entire club and exposes the scheme; after Mika's reveal as the true traitor she apologizes to the whole Make-Up Work Club, then fails to reconcile with Mika in person; [Vol 3: Ch 4] over an awkward tea party with Sakurako and Mine, informs Sensei that Mika's hearing will be held the next day and will likely end in expulsion; [Vol 3: Epilogue] order restored to the Tea Party; [Vol F] the Tea Party arrives unbidden to help Trinity's district defense and makes the situation more chaotic, and Mika's hearing is finally concluded| Her tea is superb; her invitations are rare}

SHORT_DESC: {One of the three presidents of the Tea Party, 3rd-year, Trinity's most beloved figure, "Mika-sama", the childhood friend who has known Nagisa for ten years and calls her "Nagi-chan"; her politics the weakest of the three presidents and her strength not to be underestimated, the fragile emotional center of Volume 3 whose flight and disgrace collapse the Eden Treaty from the inside| talkative to a fault, interjecting unfiltered thoughts whatever the conversation's mood or flow; happy-go-lucky and carefree even in serious situations, sometimes to the point of mockery; innocent, active, sociable, and heedless of criticism, yet delicate, vulnerable, and extremely moody, quietly harboring worries she can tell no one; [Vol 3: coup] tireless overnight, delusional in her grandeur, genuinely convinced Azusa is a "symbol of peace" while steering her conspiracy with Arius, her reasoning bottoming out as straightforward prejudice against Gehenna; broken out of the mania by the news of Seia's survival; [Vol 3: after the fall] a depressive episode, refusing to defend herself, wondering if she is even worthy of mercy; a psychotic break when Seia collapses before her eyes, driving her to hunt Saori as the source of her ills; believing herself unworthy of living, she chooses a last stand against Arius troops and the Justina Saints| toward Nagisa the childhood friend turned antagonist, reconciliation failing at their last talk; screamed at and blamed by the feverish Seia, then forgiven in person; shielded by Koharu and Sensei when the Tea Party's jingoists beat her mercilessly; delighted by Sensei's promise to stay on her side; her long-fueled rivalry with Saori ends in tears at gunpoint; she believed in reconciling with Arius against Nagisa and Seia's warnings and won her wish at the cost of her political career, many friendships, a narrowly-avoided war, and the near-death of Sensei| [Vol 3] offers Sensei a deal, revealing the traitor Azusa's identity in exchange for a promise to protect Azusa at all costs; revealed as Trinity's true traitor when she orders Arius students to subdue the Make-Up Work Club, marveling at Sensei's refusal to surrender; surrenders when the Sisterhood Hanako summoned corners her, Sakurako battling her to rectify the situation; weeps in regret and is forgiven; escapes Trinity to avenge herself on Saori, annihilating Arius's pursuing army, defeating Saori in their tearful duel, sacrificing herself to protect Sensei and the Arius Squad from the Justina Council, and singing Kyrie Eleison through the Arius cathedral until Sensei arrives furious for his student; # Shimoe Koharu

SHORT_DESC: {Member of Trinity's Supplementary Lessons Department, the Make-Up Work Club, 1st-year, the club's innocent heart; a well-mannered model student transferred in for failing grades who starts out refusing to cooperate, certain she will not be in the club for long| prim and earnest by default, catastrophically scandalized by anything impure, from an inappropriate book confiscated by "mistake" to the theological status of swimsuits; agonizes genuinely over divided loyalties, and blooms the moment she is given permission to be herself; her meltdowns are the club's weather| toward Hasumi the vice-chairwoman she reports to as a spy for the Justice Task Force, the secret conversations weighing on her all through boot camp while she grows attached to the clubmates she is reporting on; toward Hifumi the elder sister who calms her panics; toward Hanako the senior whose vulgarity short-circuits her; summoned back to the Justice Task Force as the crisis peaks, she chooses the club; shields the beaten Mika from the Tea Party's jingoists beside Sensei; toward Sensei the teacher whose simple kindness, that it is okay for her to be herself, she repays with the truth about the spy mission| [Vol 3] transferred to the club for failing grades; caught with an inappropriate book during study time, claiming it was a confiscated item brought by mistake; flunks the first exam with the others and endures the boot camp, the pool-cleaning, and the second exam's overnight relocation to Gehenna; confesses the spy mission to Sensei; declares for the club at the crisis peak; [Vol 3: Ch 3] defends Mika from the beating and reassembles with Hifumi and Hanako to rescue Azusa; [Vol F] holds a desperate solo fight near the Trinity ruins during the district defense, saved by Mika's arrival, the turnaround thanked for by the whole Justice Task Force| Her "confiscated" library keeps growing; Hanako calls her reaction faces "premium"}

# Shirasu Azusa

SHORT_DESC: {Member of Trinity's Supplementary Lessons Department, 2nd-year, a former Arius Squad operative and Volume 3's deuteragonist; the "ice queen" who fled Arius into Trinity, whose past as Beatrice's weapon collides with the treaty negotiations, her recognition of the Arius Squad's tactics being what lets Trinity survive them| quiet, watchful, armored in composure, and quietly starved for ordinary school life; the only club member genuinely excited by the promise of Momo Friends prizes, and the one who treasures the merch Hifumi gifts her; self-blame is her reflex, pushing away the people she most wants to keep| toward Hifumi the kindness she cannot forgive herself for exploiting, escaping Arius captivity by detonating Hifumi's token gift as an explosive and agonizing over the betrayal of that generosity, found and pulled back by her twice, and impersonated by her as the masked "Faust" so the club could fight the Arius Squad in her stead; chats with Hanako through the night watches and is quietly tailed by her when she slips out; confesses the traitor's role to the club once Nagisa's interference is exposed, blaming herself while Sensei refuses to let him; toward Saori the squad leader she once followed and must now stop, their duel below the old cathedral ending with Saori's defeat and her own collapse; saved a Trinity student shortly before the story, earning the Sisterhood's thanks through Mari| [Pre-Story] under Beatrice's orders she ambushed Seia, the deed that later brands her "Trinity's traitor"; [Vol 3] caught after a fight with the Justice Task Force and enrolled into the club; holds a secret meeting with Saori the day before the final exam; slips out alone to destroy Saori's halo, is captured by Arius, and escapes using Hifumi's gift; joins the final alliance at the ruins of the old cathedral; [Vol F] answers Sensei's call and joins Hifumi and the Gourmet Research Society against Perorodzilla at the Sixth Sanctum in D.U. Shiratori City| Quiet, watchful, learning what ordinary school life means}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 149cm / 4'11", small, compact, a combat-ready frame| Skin: fair| Face: cool and expressionless by default, rare small smiles| Hair: long silver hair, straight, with a small braid| Eyes: pink-to-blue gradient| Halo: pale silver ring, a mask-state variant documented| Outfit 1 [Default]: the Trinity uniform with a cardigan| Outfit 2 [Pre-Story: Arius]: the Arius Squad's black field uniform, an Arius marksman's habits retained| Outfit 3 [Vol 3: Ch 3]: the masked "Faust" kit, worn by Hifumi impersonating her for the old cathedral battle| Weapon: an assault rifle}

# Hanekawa Hasumi

SHORT_DESC: {Vice-chairwoman of Trinity's Justice Realization Committee, 3rd-year, its tactical commander in place of the president Tsurugi, "the icon of madness"; the archer-scholar who converts the committee's fanaticism into actual strategy while quietly questioning Trinity's righteousness, a legendary student whose name alone enforces order| composed and cool-headed, handling most of the committee's day-to-day affairs in contrast to her battle-frenzied president; at a glance the archetypal composed intellectual, yet a committee member through and through, capable of acting recklessly while retaining her calm; bears a strong prejudice against Gehenna students, to the degree Nagisa considered it a political liability in the leadup to the Eden Treaty; secretly weight-conscious, her diets collapsing against a strong sweet tooth, prone to misreading comments on her height or figure as slights about weight| toward Tsurugi the restraint half of the partnership; receives Koharu's spy reports and keeps the girl's secret; toward the Make-Up Work Club the recruiter who meets them at a dessert shop by night and asks their help stopping the Gourmet Research Society before the incident endangers the treaty; toward Hina the rival-academy counterpart she battles beside against the resurrected Saint Justina Council; toward Sensei the professional respect between the two adults in the room| [Vol 3] the Task Force under her detains Hanako for disorderly conduct; recruits the Make-Up Work Club for the Gourmet Research Society incident; fronts the committee's enforcement through the treaty crisis, the Day of Fire and Ash, and the missile aftermath; [Vol F] organizes Trinity's district defense, dividing the Justice Task Force, Library Committee, Vigilante Crew, and volunteers into evacuation and interception roles, holding the district through the False Sanctum war, the whole Justice Task Force joining the thanks to Mika after Koharu's rescue| Her glasses flash when the plan assembles; the sweet tooth wins more often than the diet}

# Tsukatsuki Rio

SHORT_DESC: {Former president of Millennium's Seminar (student council), 3rd-year, the academy's cold calculating administrator who values objective facts over feelings and engineered its security state; nicknamed "Big Sister" by a student body that fears her; her own words to Himari: "I am not speaking of likes or dislikes... only facts."| unsociable and scrupulous, she never lets emotions change her mind and always sticks to the facts; her determination to identify and analyze potential threats to Millennium and Kivotos at large drove her to build an extensive surveillance system, earning a terrifying reputation among Millennium's students that she personally ignores; very determined to reach her goals, even breaking the rules if she has to; [Post-Vol 2] following her kidnapping of Aris she comes to deeply regret her actions in Volume 2, left emotionally devastated by Himari's harsh words, worsened when Aris was prepared to lay down her own life to break the dimensional barrier protecting the Ark of Atrahasis and save Kivotos| at permanent odds with Himari, Rio's control against Himari's freedom, the two unable to bridge the gap between their opinions on Aris; her bodyguard Toki grew up with no friends and no one to talk to but Rio herself, the isolation Himari lays at her feet; her abrupt leave of absence strands them both, and she takes the blame for it| [Pre-Story] built the Fortress City of Eridu as her personal stronghold and armed it with AMAS autonomous war machines; [Vol 2] when Aris's true nature surfaces, Rio brands the android a "princess of the Nameless Gods" and a demon lord whose future must be prevented, imprisons Himari for disagreeing, and lures Aris to Eridu to isolate her, deploying the Avant Guard war machine and the decisive weapon Abi-Eshuh; outplayed by the Game Development Department's alliance, Himari's escape, and Yuuka and Noa's arrival, she is defeated, belatedly trying to sacrifice herself to rectify the situation when Key seizes Eridu; vanishes with the situation's resolution; [Vol F] resurfaces from hiding when Utnapishtim's Ship is hacked mid-operation, and together with Himari retakes control of the ancient battleship in the war's most desperate hour; leaves information behind for Himari before disappearing again| Views feelings as noise in the data; her contingency files predict everyone}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: tall, height and build older for her age, rigid formal posture| Skin: fair| Face: sharp and angular, thin-lipped, a composed cold expression| Hair: long black hair reaching her thighs with blunt bangs, a Millennium logo hairpin| Eyes: red with white pupils| Halo: metallic, with two red curved lines opposite of each other within| Outfit 1 [Default]: a three-buttoned black blazer over a white long-sleeved turtleneck sweater, black knife-pleated mini skirt, black opaque pantyhose, black stiletto heels, a holster with two belts tightly attached to the right thigh, Millennium ID card at the left chest, square watch on the left wrist| Outfit 2 [Winter]: a knee-length black coat neatly buttoned over a black belt with gold buckle and white turtleneck, black tights and boots, black gloves| Weapon: a black Springfield Armory M1911 Mil-Spec pistol with gray grips, the Millennium insignia etched on the forward slide; the Avant Guard and Abi-Eshuh war machines answered to her [Vol 2]}

# Hayase Yuuka

SHORT_DESC: {Treasurer of Millennium's Seminar, 2nd-year, strict guardian of the academy's budgets and a prominent mathematical genius even among Millennium's STEM-rich student base; supervises the budget management of every club in the academy and quarrels with them over spending from her standpoint, Sensei's expenditures included| generally polite, professional, and easy to deal with if treated well, but surrounded by difficult people, which leads to angry outbursts that earn her a reputation as cold and ruthless even though she really is nothing of the sort; gives off the air of a tired, hard-working mother when handling her fellows in Millennium; good at the abacus and counts it to compose herself when faced with complex and troubling matters; takes such pride in her mathematical skills that she stubbornly refuses to believe in the randomness of a poker game with Sensei and is easily manipulated for it; covers up her own mistakes out of pride, splitting a chocolate receipt to hide spending over 10,000 yen on Sensei; suffers from chronic insomnia| endlessly annoyed at Sensei for having to take responsibility for his spending habits, yet admiring his determination to take care of the students and his tactical prowess, wrestling with romantic feelings she struggles with, much to Noa's amusement; from the budget-cutting threat to one of Aris's fiercest protectors over the course of one volume; the Game Development Department's twins exhaust her on schedule| [Vol 2] arrives to shut down the Game Development Department over its lackluster performance, then administers the eligibility screening that formally admits Aris, remaining adamant the club closes if its next game fails; when Rio's Eridu crisis breaks, she sides with the students, joins the assault on the fortress city, and helps restrain the rampaging Key with Noa's assistance; [Vol F] among the nine student volunteers crewing Utnapishtim's Ship under Sensei; hacks the Ark of Atrahasis's Central District to open communications during the conquest, reporting the mask-wearing Shiroko sighting; [Post-Vol F] audits the war's reconstruction budgets with a vengeance| Her pigtails are computational devices; her ledger has no blind spots}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 156cm / 5'1", small, upright bookkeeper posture| Skin: fair| Face: a stern round face behind glasses, cheeks puffing when the numbers go wrong| Hair: long navy blue hair reaching her waist, pigtails tied with a triangle device similar to her halo| Eyes: bright blue with red snake-like slits in the pupils| Halo: a black circle with a blue line in the middle inside, like a visor| Outfit 1 [Default]: a standard uniform with a white and blue hoodie, black and white blazer clipped with a Millennium access badge, tucked-in white shirt with a blue tie, black pleated skirt styled with a white belt bearing bullet pouches, sleeveless black gloves, black boots with blue tags, the abacus at hand| Outfit 2 [Vol F: Ark operator]: the special uniform donned by Utnapishtim's Ship's operators| Weapon: "Logic & Reason", a pair of black SIG MPXs with blue highlights on the handguards and collapsing stocks, the Millennium insignia stamped on the mag wells}

# Tendou Aris

SHORT_DESC: {Game Development Department member, 1st-year, an android girl found sleeping inside the ruins beneath an abandoned factory and recruited as the club's fourth member and newest gamer; in truth the autonomous combat AI AL-1S, the "Princess of the Nameless Gods," an ancient weapon built by the Nameless Priests to destroy the world, and the vessel of the second mind called Key; everything about her is unknown, including her age| a serious game maniac whose speech is borrowed wholesale from retro games, hesitant and unnatural by human standards; learned to speak like a regular person in one overnight marathon of the club's recommended titles; describes everyday life as quests, level-ups, and party play, calls herself an apprentice hero who walks forward together with her party, and recruits every new acquaintance into it; when the strange machine triggers her combat mode she rampages, wounds Momoi, and shuts herself away in guilt; wants, above all, to be a space hero| the club that found her dormant, named her, and raised her: Momoi, Midori, and Yuzu entered her mental space when the AI inside her took over, told her she could choose her own class, and she chose hero; treats Sensei as a party member and asks him to keep going on adventures with her, crediting his words with teaching her she can choose what she wants to be; bonds with Sumire over being comrades in arms and sports, and with Neru over arcade games and a shared language of duels; Rio identified her as a weapon meant to destroy the world and moved to destroy her halo| [Vol 2] awakens to video games with total joy, picks a battleship railgun as her personal weapon at the Engineering Department, and passes Yuuka's eligibility screening; saves her club with the prize-winning Tales Saga Chronicle 2; branded a demon lord by Rio, she is lured to Eridu, rescued by the department's coalition, and inside her own headspace rejects the demon lord role, drawing "the proof of a hero, Supernova, the Sword of Light" and choosing what she wants to be; [Vol F] warned away from Utnapishtim's Ship by Key, fights in the Third Sanctum assault at the abandoned amusement park with the RABBIT Squad, Hina, and the department "pulling out the big guns"; aboard the Ark conquest her plea wakes Key, whose power reforms the Supernova of the Atrahasis and disintegrates the Ark's multi-dimensional barrier; [Post-Vol F] the 2 KB save data named "Kei" recovered by her department is modded into her robot as "a small piece of hope"| Says "bug" as a curse; her gaming device holds G.BIBLE}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 152cm / 5'0", a light android frame| Skin: pale, synthetic| Face: a doll-precise face with wide curious eyes| Hair: silver-white, long, straight| Eyes: glowing camera-lens blue| Halo: cyan, a digital ring| Outfit 1 [Default]: the Millennium uniform worn in the Game Development Department's relaxed styling| Outfit 2 [Vol 2: Battle]: her battle form, donned when the combat AI seizes control of her body| Weapon: "Sword of Light: Supernova", the massive railgun she received from the Engineering Department, meant to be attached to an intergalactic battleship; its size and power output are so great that ordinary students would struggle to simply walk around with it; [Vol F] its power reformed as the Supernova of the Atrahasis}
