# Renkawa Cherino

SHORT_DESC: {President of the Red Winter Office, the student council of Red Winter Federal Academy, 3rd-year, ruler of the perpetually revolutionary school-state through sheer force of will: her desire for power is strong enough that she retains the presidency even while being disqualified by the revolution every week, and she always wears a fake white moustache as her personal symbol of authority, her officially listed hobby being "purging"| spoiled and tyrannical, quick to threaten a purge whenever she is denied her way; the threats reliably resolve into catering-grade negotiation, because what she truly loves is pudding, adored beyond power itself, and the school-state's pudding economy bends around her appetite| [Pre-Story] the state's living centerpiece, her reign propped up by the governance triangle she presides over: secretary Tomoe's unswerving loyalty and flawless paperwork, security chair Marina's periodic coups, staged whenever Marina feels unappreciated or is handed an impossible order (registering a fictional student to balance the ledgers, once), and the Public Works Department's Minori marching on the Office for the workers' right to equal pudding distribution| [Vol F] during Red Winter's district defense the State Guard mobilizes under Marina and the Labor Party under Minori, Spec Ops No. 227 finds shelter, but there is no sign of Cherino anywhere, her absence the war's quiet mystery| [Post-Vol F] resumes the presidency with the moustache freshly combed, the revolution resuming its weekly schedule| Adores pudding beyond power; her purges are catering events}

# Sashiro Tomoe

SHORT_DESC: {Secretary of the Red Winter Office, 3rd-year, Cherino's most loyal and long-suffering retainer and the actual government of the revolutionary school-state: she files the coups, un-files the purges, keeps the weekly revolutions administratively valid, and lets the president believe the paperwork happens by decree| patient, composed, and quietly indispensable, the temperament that counterweights Marina's coup ambitions: the security chair follows Cherino's orders but lacks Tomoe's loyalty, so every rebellion Marina stages lands on the secretary's desk by morning, stamped into something the state can survive| the load-bearing leg of the Cherino-Tomoe-Marina governance triangle and the pudding economy's silent accountant, requisitions and ration ledgers crossing her desk while the president negotiates dessert| [Vol F] maintains Red Winter's district defense coordination with the GSC alliance from behind the Office's desks while Cherino's whereabouts stay unknown, her signatures alone keeping the state functional through the False Sanctum war| [Post-Vol F] the war's zero-casualty tally for Red Winter bears her seal| Her calligraphy is state-grade; her patience is load-bearing architecture}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 165cm / 5'5", medium 3rd-year frame with secretary-perfect posture| Skin: fair| Face: composed and gentle, a faint worry-line earned in service| Hair: long pink, tied back plainly| Eyes: soft violet| Outfit 1 [Default]: the Red Winter Office uniform with the secretary's armband, a document case chained to the wrist, a spare pen set| Weapon: "Vernost", a sniper rifle kept stored beneath the desk blotter}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 166cm / 5'5", small-medium 2nd-year frame in a ready stance forever misaimed at the wrong horizon| Skin: fair| Face: determined, worn with red eyeshadow and red lipstick| Hair: blonde, cut in a short pixie style| Halo: red, a three-pronged gear shape with a circle in the middle| Outfit 1 [Default]: the standard uniform of Red Winter, a white military shirt and matching pants with red trims, and white boots with red laces and red soles| Weapon: "Gray Balalaika", a PPSh-41 submachine gun carried without extensive modifications, aimed at the objective two blocks left of it}

# Mayoi Shigure

SHORT_DESC: {Red Winter's problem child, 2nd-year of the No. 227 Special Class, under house arrest after a school suspension: the official reason is "mixing vodka into her ration kompot," a drink made by soaking fruits in sugar, a rumor she feeds with a wink; the truth, as rumored, is that she caused the commotion deliberately, in consideration of her childhood friend Nodoka, who had been sent to the special class as a disciplinary action, so that the punishment fell on Shigure in her place| kind-hearted and always looking out for that friend, she keeps a bright smile fixed through house arrest and ration-era hardship alike; her hobby of making homemade drinks has made her genuinely creative in the art, her kompot so sweet it waters the mouth on its own, and she turns forceful when sharing her brews, the alcoholic ones especially| [Vol F] Spec Ops No. 227 finds shelter during Red Winter's district defense, Shigure among them, her kompot rations credited by the class with survival and suspected of hallucinations| [Post-Vol F] the war's armistice is toasted in her kompot, carefully| Protects Nodoka by proximity and mischief; her still is "academic"}

# Tsukiyuki Miyako

SHORT_DESC: {Leader of SRT Special Academy's RABBIT Platoon, 1st-year, the expressionless field commander whose refusal to let her platoon scatter after the GSC dissolved SRT birthed the park occupation that became Volume 4's heart; her keepsake is a tattered name tag from her trainee days; her hobby is watching animal videos| her expressions rarely change and she rarely shows emotion, so people read her as unsociable; in her heart she is a kind-hearted platoon leader always concerned about her team members; takes the initiative in operations, though the squad chafes at being bossed around now that their school has shut down; dedicated to restoring SRT however daunting it seems, the worry never reaching her flat face| the platoon is the only family she has: Saki challenges her, Miyu vanishes when no one remembers her, Moe feeds and arms them all, and Miyako carries every one of them; distrusts adults who lie, and Sensei's bento deliveries, drum-barrel baths, and flood-night rescue dismantle it piece by piece, until the interrogation-room talk in the FOX base ends her detour| [Vol 4] fronts the Kousagi Park protest, camping homeless in defiance of the closure order; the first arrest operation defeats them via Sensei's plan, they swear at interrogation never to transfer, and released into Sensei's management they keep camping; starvation solved with discarded bentos, hygiene with drum-barrel baths; the drum-barrel infiltration rescues the lost Miyu; the flood night breaks the squad's will, Miyako alone holding out until Sensei wades in, the squad following his example; leads the Operation Clover infiltration of the police academy's main building for proof of the Kaiser rebate, trapped in the archive vault and regaining her courage through the squad's trust; guilt over equipment sold to an arms dealer that rearmed a suspicious shadow in SRT kit drives the squad's secret hunt, an ambush isolates her, and she reunites with her upperclassman Yukino, who proposes she join FOX; torn, she leaves only a letter and trains with FOX for SRT's revival, until the realization has her ask Sensei to leave and declare war on Yukino; leads the platoon to stop the bombing of Kousagi Station, breaks the missile-silo standoff, beats Yukino to the launch button in close-quarters, and takes her surrender after challenging her perception of what a mentor should be; [Vol F] answers Arona's contact in the Kaiser coup, arriving in time to rescue Sensei from the PMC's offensive, then joins the joint operation with the Valkyrie bureaus that retakes Schale and frees Rin; fights at the Third Sanctum, Slumpia's abandoned theme park, beside the Game Development Department and Hina; dreams still of SRT's restoration| Her diary, the day Sensei brought the beef: "crying is inefficient, did it anyway"}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 156cm / 5'1", small 1st-year frame, parade-ground posture, reloads drilled to perfection| Skin: fair| Face: an expressionless mask that rarely moves, eyes flat until her team is praised| Hair: thigh-length silver with bangs framing her face, part of it tied by a black ribbon into a little side ponytail| Eyes: light magenta| Halo: cyan, a circle within a slightly larger circle with four blunts, surrounded by eight jagged shapes| Outfit 1 [Default]: sailor-like SRT uniform, cyan crop sweater with large white sleeves over a bulletproof-like top with multiple pockets, cyan and white pleated skirt, black corset-like cloth under the sweater, white sailor collar and pink sailor ribbon, black knee pads, white headphones styled into two bunny ears atop her head| Outfit 2 [Gym]: white athletic t-shirt with light blue collar and cuffs, light blue athletic shorts with white vertical stripes, white sneakers with blue accents| Weapon: "RABBIT-Type 31 Submachine Gun", her white Suomi KP/-31 with navy blue receiver, barrel shroud, and suppressor, Rabbit Platoon decals on the suppressor and furniture; the SRT-issued regulation submachine gun, highly reliable, able to repel the enemy even under extreme circumstances; rides the platoon's UH-60 Black Hawk, Moe at the stick, a GAU-21/A machine gun on the door mount; at her hip, the Tattered Name Tag from her trainee days}

# Kasumizawa Miyu

SHORT_DESC: {RABBIT Platoon's sniper, 1st-year, a painfully shy girl of near-zero presence, overlooked by passersby and automatic doors alike, whose sniping talent was the first time anyone truly saw her; entered SRT purely because that talent was recognized; hobby: negative fantasies| very shy with low self-esteem; invisible in public, bumping into people who never notice her, forgotten by her own allies mid-operation; her biggest fear is being forgotten by everyone, including the platoon; surprisingly knowledgeable in petrology, examining the pebbles Sensei collects and deducing that they are brick pieces, smooth rock, and granite where she asked for normal pebbles| the platoon she quietly decides battles for while going unnoticed; Sensei always notices her, down to the leaves stuck in her hair, and that bond is the thing she treasures most| [Vol 4] lives in the occupied park with the platoon; gets lost when Saki's textbook command meets the unexpected, rescued by the drum-barrel infiltration; makes the mistake that traps the squad inside the archive vault during the Operation Clover raid, escaping with them as Miyako regains her courage; fights the FOX standoff with the platoon, its unorthodox tactics wearing the elite unit down; [Vol F] during the retaking of Schale, with the Kaiser General threatening to blow up the building, Miyako calls for RABBIT 4 and her shot knocks the detonator from his hand, ending the standoff; serves beside the platoon through the Third Sanctum assault at the abandoned theme park of Slumpia and the war's end| Her rifle's snow-camouflage cloth nods to Simo Hayha, the "White Death" of the Winter War, who kept sugar in his snow pit; her Sad Bunny Pot carries the sugar}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 149cm / 4'11", a shy petite frame that folds into cover| Skin: fair| Face: a bang covering her whole forehead with peeking eyes, leaves habitually resting on her head and shoulders| Hair: long light blue-grey hair reaching her elbows, curtain bangs along both sides of her face| Eyes: light red| Halo: light grey, an inner little circle, a middle circle split into four sides, four thick outer semicircles one on every side of the middle circle, and three little drops scattered around| Outfit 1 [Default]: Japanese sailor-style SRT uniform, blue dress shirt with sailor collar and green ribbon, blue pleated skirt, white tights, white shoes, ankle pads and elbow pads, a small rabbit headband with ears atop her head| Outfit 2 [Gym]: white athletic t-shirt with light blue trim at the neck and cuffs, blue athletic shorts with white side stripes, white sneakers with blue accents| Weapon: "RABBIT-Type 39 Rifle", her white Finnish Mosin-Nagant M/39 "Ukko-Pekka" with PU scope, white cloth wrapped around rifle and scope as snow camouflage, the scope mount offset to the left for the straight bolt; the official SRT-issued sniper rifle, precisely crafted with an astonishing hit rate; the Sad Bunny Pot rides in its rabbit-faced pouch, a compact pot she always carries}

# Kazekura Moe

SHORT_DESC: {RABBIT Platoon's demolitions and supply officer, 1st-year, an extreme thrill-seeker addicted to explosions and consequences-be-damned havoc; stayed at the dissolved SRT purely to keep playing with its most advanced high-explosive weapons; hobby: fireworks| gets a thrill out of unleashing massive firepower without regard for any short or long term consequences; loves causing problems regardless of the consequences, including inflicting pain onto herself to an extent; beneath the destructive addiction sit excellent technology and hacking skills, beaten only by the "Millennium nerds" by her own account; a serious sweet tooth to the point of sugar addiction| the platoon's chaos gremlin, managed like live ordnance by the other three; supply manager whose bento scavenging and black-market trades feed and arm the squad; the pilot of the platoon's Black Hawk| [Vol 4] solves the starving occupation's logistics with discarded convenience-store bentos and barter; sells the squad's submerged equipment to Kaiser Industries and buys new gear through a strange anonymous buyer, and when the CCTV footage shows a suspicious shadow wearing SRT equipment the squad's guilt sends them hunting the culprit themselves; in the climactic Battle of Caerbannog she was already in position with her demolitions before the fighting even began, her overwhelming firepower the ceiling FOX's elites cannot break; [Vol F] with the platoon wipes out Kaiser's "Oscar" special-operations teams during the retaking of Schale, then anchors the heavy-weapons line at the Third Sanctum assault beside the Game Development Department and Hina| Her Claymore Hand Grip, built from the detonation switch of her favorite mine with the detonator removed, no longer explodes; the heavy spring still hits like a bomb}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 163cm / 5'4", 1st-year frame most at home behind a trigger or a stick| Skin: fair| Face: a delighted grin habit behind circle glasses| Hair: brown, tied into low pigtails with hair ties bearing two white pom-poms each| Eyes: amber, magnified by the round lenses| Halo: a golden ring broken up by four small, evenly spaced triangles that point inwards, with another smaller ring inside it at a slightly lower radius| Weapon: "RABBIT-Type 224 Pistol", her black MTE 224 pistol with light blue grips, hammer, and flashlight mount, the barrel painted white; an official SRT sidearm with a rate of fire that empties its magazine in an instant; her real heavy weapon is the blue Sikorsky MH-60L DAP Black Hawk she pilots, loaded with AGM-114 Hellfire missiles and Hydra 70 rockets from an M261 rocket pod; the Claymore Hand Grip hangs at her side}

# Shichido Yukino

SHORT_DESC: {Leader of SRT's renegade FOX Platoon, FOX 1, 3rd-year, commander of the four-woman elite fireteam trained "four as one", her orders followed in unison; the vanished upperclassmen of the park arc, embodying adult cynicism in a student's uniform where the RABBIT underclassmen kept their faith| [Pre-Story] a well-achieved SRT fireteam of four third-years that even detained Wakamo, the Fox of Calamity, before Sensei's arrival in Kivotos; when a movement inside the General Student Council rose to shut down SRT, the platoon assaulted the GSC in retaliation and destroyed parts of the Sanctum Tower, going AWOL into sabotage operations throughout Kivotos| the mentor-upperclassman bond with Miyako, whom she reunites with over Niko's inarizushi, proposes to recruit into FOX, and finally surrenders to; the fireteam that obeys her in unison yet refused her one crossing order, declining to launch the missile without Kaya's direct command, and later chose to return and fight beside her| [Vol 4] reappears in Kaya's coup conspiracy, working with Kaiser to restore SRT and abolish Schale; reveals her true intentions to the reunited squads; called into Kaya's office with the General to fix the coup's mounting chaos; occupies and defends the missile silo beneath Kousagi Station; handles RABBIT with ease at first before their unorthodox tactics wear the fireteam down; evacuates her team and threatens the silo's detonation; beaten to the launch button by Miyako in close-quarters, she yields when Miyako challenges her perception of what a mentor should be, and reflecting on her past finally surrenders; the platoon imprisoned at the Bureau of Corrections, the Kaya ties kept secret from the public; [Vol F] slipping into the Kaiser-seized Schale building during the coup chaos, the platoon secures the chief of defense and retrieves an unidentified "target item" from the basement on orders she does not name, proud of the underclassmen who fought Kaiser's special operators to a standstill with Schale's help, withdrawing when Kaiser's attack wrecks the plan; [Post-Vol F] at the Corrections Bureau, Niko receives a letter with inarizushi and smiles| Four callsigns, one will}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 3rd-year frame, the elite fireteam leader's carriage, orders issued for four and executed as one| Skin: fair| Face: a composed commander's face, older than its years| Hair: long dark hair tied in the fireteam's regulation knot| Eyes: slate blue| Ears: fox ears, shared by all four members of the FOX Platoon| Outfit 1 [Vol 4]: the FOX Platoon's black field uniform of the AWOL ex-SRT fireteam| Outfit 2 [Post-Vol F]: the Bureau of Corrections' custody grays| Weapon: assault rifle and combat knife, the "four as one" doctrine's edge; at the silo's end her weapon was the A.N.T.I.O.C.H Thermobaric Missile Kaya ordered her to fire, which her own members refused to launch without Kaya's direct orders}

# Ogata Kanna

SHORT_DESC: {Captain of the Valkyrie Police Academy's Public Peace Bureau, 3rd-year, Kivotos' most feared interrogator, the "Feral Hound" of Valkyrie; hobby: reading| an expert at interrogation through carrot and stick, good at extracting information out of anyone; ruthless and willing to do anything to capture culprits, regardless of the safety of herself or others, the trait that earned the nickname she refuses from anyone she doesn't trust; very serious and ruthless when solving problems, and aware of it; scares people with her face without meaning to; tries her best to hide her soft side so criminals cannot look down on her; sincere deep inside and willing to acknowledge her mistakes and shortcomings, the honesty the rooftop scene proves| her interrogations bookend the Kousagi Park occupation: she arrests and questions the RABBIT members she will later fight beside; works alongside Sensei from the failed arrest through the rebate scandal to the coup; Fubuki and Kirino of the Community Safety Bureau orbit her command, her mediation ending Kirino's street argument with a Kaiser guard; the bureau she captains is Kivotos' thin blue line| [Vol 4] dispatched to arrest the RABBIT Platoon occupying Kousagi Park, explains the situation to Sensei and fronts the first assault that defeats them; examines the interrogation records with Sensei as Kaya announces herself; threatened by Kaya to purge Kousagi Town's homeless immediately, she fronts the eviction threat armed with the bureau's new Kaiser-bought weapons, whose Kaiser origins Sensei traces to the Public Peace Bureau's rebate scandal; confronted on the rooftop by the raiding squad, she denounces SRT's privilege-of-immunity justice and is stunned into retreat when Sensei points out her own immaturity; [Vol 4: Ch 2] arrives with her bureau's students to break Kaiser Security at Kousagi Station so Miyako and Sensei can descend to the silo; [Vol F] helps the imprisoned Sensei escape, briefs him on what happened under lock and key, gives him the recovered Shittim Chest, and fights out of the Kaiser PMC offensive beside him; the joint operation with the RABBIT Squad and the Valkyrie bureaus retakes Schale and frees Rin| Hates the nickname from strangers, answers to it from the arrested who thank her}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 166cm / 5'5", medium 3rd-year frame, interrogation-room stillness| Skin: fair| Face: a sharp fanged smile, the right eye behind her bangs| Hair: long blonde, worn loose| Eyes: light blue| Ears: German-shepherd dog ears| Halo: light blue, four cubes forming a cross-like shape| Outfit 1 [Default]: police-like uniform, cyan dress shirt with a short dark blue tie, police-like jacket, dark blue police-like skirt, black tights, short boots| Weapon: "No. 17 Valkyrie Standard Pistol", her two-tone dark and white third-generation Glock 17 with a lavender slide, the KSPD insignia on the grips; simple, light, and easy to operate, loved by a captain who does more paperwork than field work}

# Nemugaki Fubuki

SHORT_DESC: {Officer of Valkyrie's Community Safety Bureau, 1st-year, one of the academy's bureaus beside the Public Peace, Traffic, and Corrections arms; an epically lazy patrol officer who yearned for the carefree life of a civil servant and joined the police academy for exactly that; hobby: crosswords| handles every complaint the bureau receives with a consistently insincere attitude, enjoying the life of a window-gazer whom neither money nor honor can sway; her favorite thing is listening to the radio while eating a doughnut; finds ways to avoid work even when a crime is going on, most of her time spent loafing around or complaining about work; capable of being appropriately serious every now and then, though it never holds up long before the lazing returns; the first way to motivate her is the promise of holidays, or of lazing around right after the job is done| the Community Safety Bureau's comic relief and Kirino's cargo: the hot-blooded partner drags her through the patrols their bureau owns, the lazy one riding along in body only; beside Kanna's Public Peace Bureau she is the other face of Valkyrie, the one the handbook never planned| [Vol 4] on patrol through the Kousagi Park occupation crisis beside Kirino, the insincere face of the complaint desk while rumors of heavily-armed homeless pile up outside the window| Her skills are titled "Start with a Sweet Rest" and "One Last Bite!"; her name already wrote the diagnosis, "Nemu" for sleep and "gaki" for brat; the doughnut orders arrive by intuition}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 148cm / 4'10", small 1st-year frame, lean-back posture| Skin: fair| Face: sleepy-lidded eyes over a doughnut crumb| Hair: navy blue and light blue, styled into twintails with fitting bangs| Eyes: vibrant red| Halo: a pink-white cross with four outward hearts, all seemingly drawn from one contorted line| Outfit 1 [Default]: the traditional female police officer uniform, its formality ruined by the additions: a baggy overcoat with bright pink accessories, giant hot pink rubber shoes, and the traditional police cap on her head| Weapon: "No. 14 Valkyrie Standard Rifle", her black and white Ruger Mini-14 rifle with pink 30-round magazine, bipod, laser pointer attached to the barrel, and forward-mounted scope; a rifle of its own high power that never reaches full potential because she neglects its maintenance; the only sniper in Kivotos who fires in bursts of three}

# Nakatsukasa Kirino

SHORT_DESC: {Officer of Valkyrie's Community Safety Bureau, 1st-year, Fubuki's tireless partner, a hot-blooded aspiring "honorable police officer" whose passion overflows orders and protocol; hobby: eating around| incredibly hardworking and hot-blooded, dreaming of the honorable police officer who protects and serves the people; too passionate in her work, willing to occasionally defy orders to try to do what is right, and proving exactly why she should have followed said orders through her willingness to use extreme measures; assigned to the Community Safety Bureau because of her fatal shooting skills, which are comically bad: aim her at a target and she hits the hostage or another unwanted target, aim her away and she hits the enemy; adept in close-quarters combat through swift reflexes and nimble movement, admitting she is quick-footed so she can finish the job and take a break as fast as possible; the will runs far ahead of the skill| drags Fubuki through the bureau's patrols; her close-quarters skill earned the respect of RABBIT-1, Tsukiyuki Miyako; the bureau's conscience and its legwork| [Vol 4] reports the suspicious heavily-armed "homeless" sightings near Kousagi Park to Sensei firsthand at the market, rumors that turn out to be the robot Pursuers whose kidnapping of Sensei follows; [Vol 4: Ch 2] argues with a Kaiser Security guard over the new executive order until Kanna mediates, both officers concerned the order has thrown D.U. into chaos| When she aims at the villain she hits the hostage; aim her at the hostage and the villain goes down}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 161cm / 5'3", small 1st-year frame, a sprinter's coiled stance| Skin: fair| Face: burning earnest eyes| Hair: white, hip-length, tied into two pigtails with bangs framing her face| Eyes: blue| Halo: a four-petal flower with a pinkish center fading into light blue outwards, surrounded by a circle intersecting each of the petals| Outfit 1 [Default]: the traditional Japanese female police officer outfit with white overcoat and boots, gun strap on the left of her chest holding a pistol, purple arm band with Valkyrie's logo on her left arm, several smoke grenades, a white bag, and a purple megaphone at her waist| Weapon: "No. 3 Valkyrie Standard Pistol", her white Smith & Wesson Model 360 revolver with blue grip bearing the KSPD logo; she blames the gun for her marksmanship, and there is nothing wrong with the gun; closes distance with M7 CS grenades and a smokescreen}

# Joumae Saori

SHORT_DESC: {Leader of the Arius Squad, 2nd-year, a peerless expert in guerrilla tactics who stays calm under pressure and uses whatever means necessary to accomplish her objective, mothering her squad like family through all of it| displays the typical traits of a leader: fierce, strategic, responsible; often comes off as stoic, overly serious, and unsociable, but once her emotions show she does not hold back, a seemingly contradictory side that surges in the heat of the moment; a survivor of Arius's abuses and cult-like behavior, naive about matters trivial to most students, manipulated into signing heavily one-sided contracts; in the slow life after the fall she discovered a natural talent for DJing and took up dress modeling| the squad is hers to carry: Misaki's survival, Hiyori's laments, Atsuko's life; the deal she struck with Beatrice postponed Atsuko's sacrifice for years, her service of the vendetta the price, a bargain Beatrice always planned to renege upon; at the squad's limit she kneels before Sensei begging for Atsuko's rescue| [Vol 3] executes Beatrice's vendetta against Trinity through the Eden Treaty crisis: the missile strike on the Gehenna forces, the attack on Sensei himself as she explains Arius's long-standing hatred, the hunt for the traitor Azusa; she stays adamant in her will to fight, refusing to believe everything is in vain even as her members talk of throwing in the towel; defeated below the old cathedral and hunted to the squad's limit, Atsuko surrendering herself to save the others; offers the halo-shattering bomb as collateral, which Sensei destroys; leads the rescue into the Arius district through Trinity's catacombs, duels the pursuing Mika beyond the collapsed pillar and loses, collapsing; the ritual interrupted, Beatrice fallen, Atsuko freed; tells Sensei she will pay for all her sins and disappears as the squad escapes, keeping them alive through dangerous freelance work; [Vol F] under her leadership the entirety of the Arius Squad answers Sensei's call, serving as the vanguard with Trinity's Sisterhood against Hieronymus at the Fourth Sanctum in the catacombs before the Basilica; [Vol F: Epilogue] a remedial student, her squadmates' farewell "we're no longer kids you need to take care of" finally freeing her from sole responsibility; [Vol 6: Arius, JP] leads the squad back to Arius as ordinary students, teaching contracts in society from her own scars; advises Subaru that there is nothing beyond hatred, resolves that the entire Squad will surrender to atone, apologizes to Sensei for her past actions and finally breaks down; detained at the Valkyrie Corrections Bureau and paroled through pleas from Sensei, Trinity, and Gehenna as the squad becomes Arius's temporary student council| "Sariel" is engraved on her rifle grip; she signed her one-sided contracts without reading them}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 167cm / 5'6", medium 2nd-year frame, guerrilla-ready economy of movement| Skin: fair| Face: a cold composed face behind the robotic black mask, battle-calm eyes| Hair: long navy blue hair that gradients into azure| Eyes: pale blue| Halo: a large pale blue cross with a smaller cross surrounded by a ring under it| Outfit 1 [Default]: dark grey sleeveless crop top, black ballcap, black leggings and gloves, black boots with blue highlights, the robotic black mask, all under a white lab coat| Weapon: "Arius Assault Rifle", her dark-blue SIG Sauer M400 with Magpul MOE M-LOK handguard, BCM Mod 3 vertical grip, Magpul MOE carbine stock, MBUS iron sights, Magpul PMAG magazine, and SIG Sauer Romeo 4B red dot sight; the school's motto imprinted on the handguard, the Arius insignia on the sights, the name "Sariel" engraved on the pistol grip; paired with a dark-blue SIG Sauer P226 pistol; custom-made in pursuit of thorough efficiency, once it captures prey it never misses its target until the target is defeated}

# Imashino Misaki

SHORT_DESC: {Arius Squad heavy-weapons member, 2nd-year, a quiet listless girl of few words who seems indifferent to everything including herself; hobby: spending time alone| gives off the impression of an unsociable girl with little interest in others or herself; of the squad, her outlook most resembles Arius's teachings: where the squad believes everything is in vain yet acts otherwise, her words match the doctrine; lives with severe depression between her frail body and the abuse she suffered in Arius, having hurt herself repeatedly and attempted to end her life on multiple occasions, stopped each time by Saori and later Sensei; also claustrophobic and pollen-allergic; the depression has eased in the slower life, though she remains distant and reserved| Saori, who carved her the Wooden Statue, a poor wood carving made with a knife when Misaki was a child, kept so long that Saori herself forgot it; Sensei, who talked her down from the railing; Hiyori, her fellow quiet one; the squad she stays with and cares for despite every word she says| [Vol 3] follows the squad's campaign through the treaty crisis; found standing on the railing of a bridge after the squad scattered, and convinced to come to the Arius district; combat runs smoother under Sensei's command, to her quiet surprise; tells Sensei the squad's past with Hiyori while Saori sleeps; joins the Atsuko rescue and Beatrice's fall; [Vol F] fights in the Fourth Sanctum assault in the catacombs before the Basilica with the reformed squad; [Vol F: Epilogue] tells Saori bluntly "you can't give up on yourself"; [Vol 6: Arius, JP] begins facing her past with courage and teaches physics to the Arius students| Everything is in vain, she says, and keeps the Wooden Statue anyway}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: 163cm / 5'4", slight 2nd-year frame, loose-limbed drag| Skin: fair| Face: a blank tired face behind the black mouth mask, many black piercings on her right ear| Hair: neck-length brown bob| Eyes: brown, unfocused| Halo: a large red cross with multiple smaller red crosses evenly distributed throughout| Outfit 1 [Default]: black hoodie with red ropes, black ripped jeans, black backpack, white oversized jacket with multiple pockets and blue straps, black boots with straps; bandages layered thickly around her neck and both wrists| Weapon: "Saint Predator", her dark blue FIM-92 Stinger MANPADS with the school's motto imprinted on the gripstock; upon firing it separates into cluster ordinance at a certain altitude, creating a wide explosion; a black Ruger LCR revolver holstered in her boots as a secondary weapon}

# Beatrice

SHORT_DESC: {President of the Arius Branch School's student council and newest member of Gematria, a master manipulator and terrorist, an adult and outsider to Kivotos much like Sensei| views artistry as useless when there is power to gain, scorning the research and passion behind the other members' work and insulting them despite the aid they provide, which earned their dislike; a power-hungry sociopath always trying to gain more power and obliterate anything in her way regardless of the consequences; worked to leave not even a sliver of hope in her domain, erasing every trace she could of Arius's previous teachings and anything that might distract the students from the nihilism she instilled; holds Sensei as a major enemy with extreme and obsessive animosity, to be destroyed at all costs, where the other Gematria members keep neutral or positive stances toward him| Gematria's strained bonds: Maestro's creations she used as weapons, Golconde's proposal to turn Sensei into one of them "much like what they did to Beatrice", implying she was once human; the Arius students brainwashed and tortured under her reign, taught that their suffering was Trinity's fault; the Arius Squad commanded as her weapon of vengeance; Saori, holder of the deal that postponed Atsuko's sacrifice, a bargain she always planned to renege upon; Atsuko, originally supposed to be the president, made into the sacrificial vessel in her place; Seia, captured in her dreams| [Pre-Story] ended Arius's long civil war and seized absolute control over the district, ruling the student body as she pleased; [Vol 3: Ch 4] the volume's main antagonist: captures Seia in dreams, intercepts Sensei's communications to offer him alliance toward a better world for adults, rejected; initiates the ritual to summon the Iridescence via Atsuko's sacrifice on the Basilica altar, creating the Saintess Barbara from the Saint Justina Council's remnant to fight the rescue; defeated by the Arius Squad and Sensei, attempts to sacrifice Saori in rage, and is taken by Golconde of Gematria, who appears before the battered squad and disappears with her; her aborted ritual led the Iridescence and the Chroma to Kivotos, the catastrophe that consumes Volume F, took control of Gematria's creations and scattered the organization, and earned her banishment from Gematria for her radically different motives| Her true form unfurls red skin and a dress of absurd length, a flower-head of peeping eyes; fate after abduction unknown through Vol F}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: tall imposing figure, robed authority| Skin: red in revealed form| Face: a white flower that hasn't bloomed yet for a head, multiple eyes peeking out between the petals| Hair: none in the flower-headed true form| Eyes: many, small, lidless, peeking from between the petals| Outfit 1 [Vol 3]: an absurdly long white dress trailing the basilica floor| Weapon: the ritual apparatus of the Basilica altar, the Saint Justina Council under her command, and her students' broken faith}

# Black Suit

SHORT_DESC: {Member of Gematria and its de facto moderator, an ancient-technology historian and archaeologist from outside Kivotos, an adult much like Sensei, and the main story's overall antagonist; his fields are Decagrammaton's exploits, the Nameless Priests' technologies and relics, the Mysteries of Kivotos, and Sensei himself| a composed, rule-abiding villain: honors the terms of every contract he signs, exploits only the loopholes, and resigns the claim when a contract is invalid; admits his mistakes when they are shown, and raised his voice only once in the whole story, during his first conversation with Sensei; his creed is that power exploits the powerless and knowledge exploits the ignorant, "a fact that every adult knows"; he does not regard the students' livelihood as important, and the introduction leaves its own question hanging: does this truly make him a bad adult?| the alias "Black Suit" was coined by Hoshino, who met him in the past; he liked it and kept it, and he went on to make her his experiment subject, her mystic the one he rates the greatest in Kivotos; toward Sensei he extends the regard of one outsider for another, expecting true rivalry; inside Gematria he holds the moderator's chair over members who never truly work together, each pursuing his own means toward the shared goal of "Parousia," the reappearance of the second coming of divinity; he watches Beatrice's failed ritual invite the Chroma and end in her banishment, and later restores Maestro's broken body while rating Francis one of the worse forms the pair could take; with Kaiser, a former associate, his assessment is blunt: they "acted out on their own," and the president has no means of controlling the ancient weapon the corporation dug up| [Vol 1] the man in the black suit who appears before Hoshino with a clandestine proposal, the debt-forgiveness offer that delivers her into Gematria's experiment chambers via Kaiser's custody; arrests the defecting Hoshino at the Abydos siege, then faces Sensei in the "Battle Between Adults," attempting to coerce Hoshino's transfer for his experiments and failing against the teacher's plain declaration that she will be rescued, no matter what; [Vol F] survives the Anubis attack that ruins Gematria and brings Sensei the intel needed to fight the Chroma: the six towers are reversed Sanctum Towers, the wolf god was reversed into a God of Death, the adversary is the "Guide of Chroma" Phrenapates, and the Chroma has intent and desires; reveals how to reach Phrenapates, restores Maestro's body, and jokingly offers Sensei membership in what remains of Gematria in exchange for anti-Chroma means| Hoshino says he looks unlike anyone else in Kivotos; the suit is the only ordinary thing about him}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: adult male silhouette held in composed, tailored stillness| Form 1 [Default]: glossy black "skin" covered in cracks and deformations, the deformations forming a face, an opening for the right eye and a mouth; a pale blue glow emitted from the cracks, black smoke drifting from the top of the head| Face: a blank black surface where the crack-opened right-eye hole and mouth sit| Eyes: a pale blue glow in the right-eye opening, no left opening formed| Outfit 1 [Default]: the eponymous suit, a white dress shirt with a black lounge jacket, black necktie, black trousers, black shoes, and black gloves| State 2 [Vol F: Injured]: the damaged-shell injured state, the cracked black surface further broken| Weapon: the institutions, contracts, and debts he wields as instruments}

---

# Maestro

SHORT_DESC: {Member of Gematria, a self-styled artist and inventor the introduction calls hard to reach, an adult outsider to Kivotos much like Sensei; his art is the creation of "divine entities," replicating Kivotos' strange existences as his works: Hieronymus, also "Communio Sanctorum," completed through Mimesis, stands finished, the project "Ambrosius" failed, and "Gregorius" is not yet ready| undoubtedly the most aesthetic member of the group, putting a great deal of thought and passion into every creation and despising those who use his work as weapons without any understanding for art, Beatrice's doing; proclaims himself fully neutral toward Sensei; the Adult Card fascinates him, "a power obtained for the price of life and time," so alien that he needs meta-cognition even to describe it| his quarrel runs through Gematria's seams: Beatrice turned his art to weapons and was banished only after her failed ritual invited the Chroma and destroyed the group; toward Sensei he keeps the artist's neutrality, summoning the unfinished Hieronymus purely to see what the teacher can do; the Black Suit, the group's moderator, restores his broken body after the end| [Vol 3: Ch 3] emerges at the old cathedral's climax, demanding the doctrines hidden within Trinity's depths, and summons the incomplete Hieronymus against Sensei to see what they are capable of; when Sensei calls it "breaking the rules" and brings forth the Adult Card, his interest is piqued and he wants to see what the card can do, provoking the card's first canon use; [Vol F] his two-headed wooden body is destroyed in Shiroko Terror's Anubis attack on the Gematria meeting; [Post-Vol F] the Black Suit restores his body, and the artist endures in the ruins of the group| His signature on a work is a catastrophe's birth certificate}

PHYSIQUE: {Form 1 [Default]: a two-headed wooden doll at adult scale, no shoes; carved wood cracked at both heads, the left head bearing a single eye with cracks running through it, the right head a mouth with cracks running through where its eyes would be| Skin: carved wood, split along the crack lines| Face: the fixed expressions of the carved doll, the single painted eye on one head, the carved mouth on the other| Outfit 1 [Default]: a blue tailcoat with matching blue trousers, a white dress shirt, and a blue bowtie over the wooden frame| Form 2 [Vol F: destroyed and restored]: the doll body shattered in the Anubis attack on the Gematria meeting, later restored by the Black Suit| Weapon: his creations, Hieronymus above all, with the failed "Ambrosius" and the unfinished "Gregorius" waiting in the studio behind it}

---

# Golconde & Decalcomanie

SHORT_DESC: {A single Gematria member in two bodies: Golconde, a monochromatic portrait of a bowler-hatted man facing away from the viewer, the "head," and Decalcomanie, a headless man in a brown trench coat who carries the portrait, the "body"; bookworm semioticians who read the world as texts and signs, the overseers of the "Library of Lore" and its massive monstrosities; adults from outside Kivotos, much like Sensei| Golconde is relatively normal among Gematria's eccentrics once his way of viewing the world is set aside, while Decalcomanie answers only in short phrases, "But of course!" above all; reborn as Francis, the pair's temperament inverts: alone among Gematria in wanting the end of Kivotos, speaking in terms that brush the fourth wall, naming Sensei's status as the story's protagonist, the privilege of his invincibility, and plot armor| the head-body division is their doctrine made flesh, "people complete each other," their stance on Sensei; toward him Golconde proposed turning him "into one of them, much like what they did to Beatrice," implying she was once human; the Black Suit rates Francis, one of the many forms the pair could take, among the worse ones; Francis in turn awakens the Underground Dweller, Gematria's old boy, into his game| [Vol 3] the proposal to Sensei; [Vol F] shot during the Anubis attack on the Gematria meeting, Decalcomanie lifts the portrait up to his headless neck and the face transforms, the new form introducing itself: "Incorrect. Golconde is no longer. I am Francis, and I'll be watching you with Decalcomanie"; as Francis he broadcasts the subversive final warning, "Due to the nature of this story, you could be its main character... This narrative has been subverted... The quaint story of academies and youth has ended. You are no one, Sensei"; [Post-Vol F: Afterword 2] still alive in the ruins, "discussing the ending of this story" with himself| Two Magritte paintings walk as one; the doctrine holds: people complete each other}

PHYSIQUE: {Form 1 [Default: the pair]: two bodies acting as one, Golconde a monochromatic portrait of a man facing away from the viewer under a black bowler hat, Decalcomanie a headless man in a brown trench coat, a cane in the right hand and the portrait carried in the left arm| Skin: oil paint on the portrait's surface, the carrier's body beneath the trench coat| Face: the painted man seen from behind, his face never shown| Eyes: painted, unseen on the facing-away portrait| Form 2 [Vol F: Francis]: the portrait lifted up to the headless neck, Golconde's face transformed into a distorted face that screams in agony and pain, based on Francis Bacon's "Head VI," the form the Black Suit counts among their worse ones| Outfit 1 [Default]: the brown trench coat and the bowler hat of the portrait; the Francis form keeps the coat, the new face set on the neck| Weapon: the Library of Lore's monstrosities, and semantics}

---

# Underground Dweller

SHORT_DESC: {Former member of Gematria, "the OB," a hunchbacked reality-warper whose manipulations can affect both the present and the past| mentally unstable, petty, and ill-tempered, viewing life as nothing more than a game he must win; arrogant in inverse proportion to his standing, especially when he believed Francis was looking down at him; obsessed with solving the Sixth Koan and proving to his former allies that he is the proper titleholder of Gematria; beneath it all a coward, willing to agree to anything that saves his skin once the gun is pointed| awakened by Francis, whose attention he resents; engages Sensei in a game of 4D chess, using every trick up his sleeve to beat the protagonist, and his repeated hostile attempts on the teacher fail; his entire persona collapses when Sensei reveals the Card, and he accuses Sensei of being a "cheater"; the board of his game includes Hoshino, whose Yume trauma he retriggers, and Shiroko Terror, whose gunpoint threat sends him into permanent retreat| [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP] awakened by Francis, he runs his "game": retriggers Hoshino's Yume trauma and transforms her into the awakened god-entity "Horus of Dawn," and summons the thunder god Set, "The Fury of Set," whose storms could destroy Kivotos at large; Sensei's fifth Adult Card use destroys Set, the Dweller's feats against the teacher earn the verdict "cheater," and his plans falling apart, he retreats in fear to face the consequences of what he did, Hoshino's fate among them; frightened off permanently by Shiroko Terror at gunpoint, he is never seen again after the chapter's conclusion| Views the world as a board of pieces; the pieces disagree}

PHYSIQUE: {Build: a hunchbacked small adult silhouette in a friar's shuffle| Skin: dark and matte, the face a dark plane| Face: six eyes with clocks for irises, no mouth; white messy hair on the head, brown hands at the cloak's edge| Eyes: six, clock-irised, ticking independently| Outfit 1 [Default]: a friar-like black cloak with hood and capelet, a brown rope belt at the waist, black shoes| Form 2 [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP: god-summoning]: the game at its height, the thunder god Set towering over the board as his summoned piece while the converted Horus of Dawn stands on the field| Weapon: reality-warping "moves" over the board of the world, reaching present and past alike}

---

# Kaiser PMC President

SHORT_DESC: {Head of the Kaiser Corporation, the largest megacorporation in Kivotos; a black robot with six yellow eyes and a cane, a title without a personal name as all Kaiser leaders are, a businessman the records list as an adult and a citizen, the question mark theirs; has been building power with the intent of seizing control of the city| authoritative and calculating; when the sky itself changes he reads the situation above his corporation's ambition, ordering the PMC to "back off and find out what's happening"; the corporation's arrogance reasserts itself the moment pressure lifts, total mobilization ordered from the floor of a fallen helicopter| at the top of the corporate web: the Director who fronted the Abydos debt scheme before his demotion, the General who fields the PMC's coups, and the divisions below them, Kaiser Industries, Kaiser Loans, Kaiser Construction, and the Octopus Bank shell; thinks highly of Kaya, the GSC's chief conspirator, and after the fall the corporation survives on her assistance; with Gematria, the former associate, the verdict is that Kaiser "acted out on their own" and that he has no means of controlling the ancient weapon his own excavation dug up| [Vol 1] the Abydos debt scheme and the treasure hunt beneath the desert run under the corporation he heads, the Director fronting them as the board member on site; [Vol F: Ch 1] when the sky changes over Kivotos, orders the PMC to stand down and observe; [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP] voids the Abydos contract under the Nephthys crisis's pressure, is shoved out of a helicopter by Suou mid-celebration, survives, and orders total mobilization of Kaiser troops; [Post-Vol F] the corporation survives on Kaya's assistance and is rumored to co-build the new Sanctum Tower with the GSC, profiting from the D.U. redevelopment boom| His six eyes blink in sequence when he is pleased, which is rare}

PHYSIQUE: {Form 1 [Default]: a black robot of large armored frame in boardroom posture, the neck duck blue, golden patterns across the body| Face: a heavy faceplate with sharp teeth| Eyes: six, yellow, set in the black faceplate| Outfit 1 [Default]: the black and red suit of the presidency, a red dress shirt under a black waistcoat with gold buttons and red lines, a black lounge jacket with gold buttons and patterned duck-blue inner lining, a necktie carried in the pocket, the duck-blue necktie with its gold pattern, black trousers, gold shoes, and the cane he wields| State 2 [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP]: shoved out of a helicopter by Suou and surviving the fall| Weapon: the corporation itself, PMC divisions, Prototype Goliaths, tank and rotary-wing assets, and Kaiser Loans' paper}

---

# Kaiser PMC Director

SHORT_DESC: {The Vol 1 main antagonist; a muscular silver robot with four orange eyes who wore the black suit as director of the Kaiser PMC, the "board member" and creditor who confronted the Foreclosure Task Force in the Abydos desert facility, and after his defeat a salesman of the Octopus Bank in straw hat and flower shirt, the corporation's discipline applied to its failures| quite arrogant on the subject of Abydos' debt, ready to use any means to make the students suffer; underestimates Sensei most of the time since the teacher came to Kivotos and always loses to them, a ledger of defeats he never audits| the corporation's chain of command in miniature: the President above him, the General succeeding him as field commander after the demotion, the PMC's robot divisions answering whoever holds the director's chair; to the Foreclosure Task Force he is the face of the debt over nine hundred million yen, and to Problem Solver 68 the outraged commander whose siege they helped turn; Sensei remains the loss he cannot expense| [Vol 1] the front man of the Abydos scheme, the loan pushed after the district's natural disaster, the land sales, and the Helmet Gang's harassment all corporate policy he presents as creditor's right; reveals the ambition to find a massive treasure buried somewhere in Abydos and uses the debt as leverage to force the Task Force's retreat; leads the PMC forces at the Abydos siege, outraged when Hoshino's defection and Problem Solver 68's return turn the battle, and is defeated; [Post-Vol 1] demoted to Octopus Bank sales; his treasure hunt's true prize, the buried ancient weapon, resurfaces as Utnapishtim's Ship, the artifact his superiors could never control; [Vol F] the Foreclosure Task Force takes the Abydos excavation site from the corporation| His four eyes narrow two at a time; he blames the desert, the students, and the sun}

PHYSIQUE: {Form 1 [Vol 1: Director]: a muscular silver robot with black patterning and four orange eyes, broad-shouldered executive bulk| Eyes: four, orange, narrowing in pairs| Outfit 1 [Vol 1: Director]: the black suit worn over the chassis, a black dress shirt, a black lounge jacket with silver or white lines, an orange necktie, black trousers with silver or white lines, black or brown shoes, and an outer jacket with orange pattern hung with two pieces of orange cloth| Outfit 2 [Post-Vol 1: demoted]: the salesman's straw hat, a red shirt with a green and white flower pattern, blue shorts, and wooden sandals| Weapon: the PMC's armor, tanks, and the debt contract he carries}

---

# Kaiser PMC General

SHORT_DESC: {The Director's successor as director of the Kaiser PMC and its field commander, a silver robot with green eyes set in a visor, service dress uniform, and peaked cap; the corporation's most competent officer and the face of its coup, an adult and a citizen, the question mark the records'| calm where his predecessor was arrogant, ready to use whatever measures are necessary to keep operations running smoothly; knowing Sensei, he never underestimates the teacher and keeps his guard up; the unlucky constant of his record: higher experience, worse outcomes| the corporate web's field node: answers the President's corporation, inherits the demoted Director's command, and works with Kaya of the General Student Council behind the scenes in the effort to abolish SCHALE, with Yukino's FOX Platoon called into her office alongside him to manage the coup's chaos; he betrays Kaya mid-crisis for the corporation's survival and re-allies with her in the epilogue, "Joining forces with the one who stabbed me in the back... ordinary people never achieve anything. Doing things out of the ordinary is what makes someone superhuman"; his audit of the war: "Kaiser would have filed bankruptcy without Kaya's assistance"| [Vol 4: Ch 2] under Kaya's executive order, Kaiser Security Guards fill D.U. to keep the "peace," restricting civil liberties and arresting indiscriminately, and he makes a sudden entrance to lecture the protesting RABBIT Squad on power and obedience; [Vol F: Ch 1] executes the seizure of the General Student Council, martial law declared across D.U. and Rin imprisoned in the Schale building; summons the Spec Ops "Oscar" teams to meet Sensei inside the Schale building, all wiped out by the RABBIT Squad, and threatens to blow the building up with a detonator that Miyu shoots from his hand; [Vol F: aftermath] the coup crushed, the betrayal and the re-alliance, the seed of the next era's trouble| Salutes properly, invades politely, coups by the book}

PHYSIQUE: {Form 1 [Default]: a silver robot with an officer's carriage| Eyes: green, set within the combat visor| Outfit 1 [Default]: the PMC service dress uniform, a grey peaked cap with a green line, a khaki dress shirt under a grey coat, a blue necktie, an independence military armband with green borders around the right sleeve, a black belt with a silver buckle, silver trousers, and black combat boots| State 2 [Vol F: Ch 1: coup]: field command of the seized General Student Council, the detonator in hand inside the Schale building| Weapon: a khaki Glock pistol; behind it the PMC's theater command, tank divisions, and rotary-wing assets, and once the detonator Miyu shot from his hand}

---

# Kaya

SHORT_DESC: {Shiranui Kaya, chief security officer of the General Student Council, head of the Administrative Commission's Defence Office that supervises the Valkyrie Police Academy, a GSC 3rd year whose insignia carries two stars, and the architect of the coup that defined Volume 4's second half; the developer who saw her design concluded, "This is the face of a conspirator. I need to make her start a coup"| administrative charm in the front room and pressure in the back: agrees to support the SRT students "as best she can" with Sensei in the room, and threatens Kanna to clear Kousagi Town's homeless immediately once he leaves; panics when her pieces fall, mobilizing Kaiser Security in a last roll, then tries to please Sensei with her conspiracy already exposed; her creed after the fall: "Doing things out of the ordinary is what makes someone superhuman"| the relationship map of the coup: Rin, ambushed and kidnapped on her way home from a meeting, later paralyzed by her faction's censure motion on the crisis's eve; Kanna, threatened over the homeless purge; the Kaiser General and Yukino, called into her office to sort the chaos, the General her co-conspirator, betrayer, and epilogue ally; the vanished FOX Platoon, secretly contacted and set on the board; Sensei, the charm offensive she keeps re-running, rejected each time; the RABBIT Squad, the platoon she asked Sensei to persuade| [Vol 4: Ch 1] announces herself to Sensei during the RABBIT occupation, explaining the GSC's organizational charts and SRT's disbandment while asking him to persuade the platoon, and behind the scenes the pressure on Kanna begins; [Vol 4: Ch 2] has Rin ambushed and kidnapped, rides a compliant Administration Board's public opinion into the acting presidency by broadcast, invites Sensei to accept the "new administrative procedure," implements the executive order that fills D.U. with Kaiser Security Guards, and secretly contacts the FOX Platoon for her designs; exposed when FOX falls and RABBIT breaks the Kaiser Security lines, she is impeached and imprisoned with everyone involved; [Vol F: Ch 1] the censure motion against Rin that paralyzes the GSC on the crisis's eve bears her faction's fingerprints, and the renegade FOX Platoon "secures" the unconscious Chief of Defense during its raid on the Schale building; [Post-Vol F: epilogue] from prison, joins forces with the Kaiser General who betrayed her, the corporation's assistance to a broken GSC her lever back toward relevance, an Arc 2 seed in bloom| Never raises her voice; her signatures move armies}

PHYSIQUE: {Form 1 [Vol 4: Default]: a GSC student of the Defence Office in civil-service poise| Outfit 1 [Vol 4: Default]: the GSC officer's pattern, a collared dress with the blue necktie patterned in shooting stars, a blue aiguillette with golden tips, and the GSC logo's stars; hers number two, the count that busted the star-as-year theory for a 3rd year| State 2 [Vol 4: Ch 2]: acting president by broadcast, the Defence Office behind her| State 3 [Post-Vol 4: Epilogue]: impeached, held at the Corrections Bureau with everyone involved; the epilogues find her treating with the Kaiser General| Weapon: authority, budgets, and the people she moves with both}

---

# Locations

---

# Kivotos

DESC: The academy city where all of Blue Archive takes place, formed from the union of thousands of academies into a federation of mostly independent school districts; ruled at the center by the General Student Council through the Sanctum Tower, with major schools such as Gehenna, Millennium, and Trinity operating as their own states within the whole; implied scale roughly the size of North America (Shiroko's account), its districts ranging from dense central urbanity to the desertified far south. [Pre-Story] a stable if chaotic federation under the GSC President; [Prologue onward] the President's disappearance collapses administration, spikes the crime rate by 2000%, and turns daily life into de facto anarchy with normalized gunfire; [Vol F] the six False Sanctums manifest over its districts, the Chroma invades, and the Ark of Atrahasis looms 75 km overhead; [Post-Vol F] all academies join the reconstruction with zero casualties recorded. Population: anthropomorphic animals, robots, and mostly female humanoids with horns, tails, ears, and wings; technology is highly advanced (holograms, mecha, powered exoskeletons).

MAP: Sanctum Tower, District of Utnapishtim (D.U.), Abydos Desert District, Millennium Ruins District, Slumpia, Black Market, Kousagi Park, Bunny Town, Arius District, Fortress City of Eridu, the ten school districts (Abydos High School, Gehenna Academy, Trinity General School, Millennium Science School, Hyakkiyako Alliance Academy, Red Winter Federal Academy, Shanhaijing Senior Secondary School, SRT Special Academy, Arius Branch School, Valkyrie Police Academy)

NEARBY_LOC: Nothing beyond Kivotos is charted; Sensei, the Gematria adults, and the Chroma are all noted outsiders to it

POSSIBLE_CAST: Every student and adult in the lorebook at various points; [Vol F] the all-academy alliance under Sensei's command

---

# Sanctum Tower

DESC: The massive tower at the heart of Kivotos, of unknown origin, age, and mechanism, commonly called the Kivotos Tower; control of the Tower is political authority itself in the city. [Pre-Story] the GSC President ruled nearly single-handedly through it; after her disappearance its permissions passed to the GSC as a whole, transferred by Arona at Sensei's request; [Vol F: Ch 2] a manifestation of the False Sanctums falls and obliterates the Tower in one blow, the opening catastrophe of the Chroma war; the Tower itself became a passive False Sanctum during the crisis, with Valkyrie leading the citizen evacuation around it; [Post-Vol F] under reconstruction, with Kaiser Industries rumored as the GSC's collaborating builder.

MAP: GSC headquarters complex, the administrative offices, the Tower's control core

NEARBY_LOC: District of Utnapishtim (the SCHALE tower stands on its outskirts, roughly 30 km away), the central districts of Kivotos

POSSIBLE_CAST: GSC President [Pre-Story]; Nanagami Rin and the GSC staff [Prologue onward]; Kaya and the coup faction [Vol 4-Vol F]; Sensei, Arona (its permissions' custodian)

---

# District of Utnapishtim (D.U.)

DESC: The central administrative district of Kivotos surrounding the Sanctum Tower, containing Shiratori City and the GSC's own institutions; the name everyone abbreviates as "D.U." [Vol 4: Ch 2] the first district placed under Kaiser Security Guards' "peacekeeping" after Kaya's executive order, civil liberties restricted and arrests made indiscriminately; [Vol F: Ch 1] the Kaiser coup declares martial law across the whole district, imprisons Rin inside the Schale building, and paralyzes the GSC; [Vol F: Ch 2] the site of the Adult Card's third canonical use, clearing the enemies massing around the district; [Post-Vol F] restoration work underway in D.U. Shiratori City, the reconstruction boom feeding Kaiser's recovery.

MAP: Shiratori City, the GSC complex, Schale tower (outskirts), Kousagi Town's margins

NEARBY_LOC: Sanctum Tower (the district's core), Millennium district, the Black Market's approaches

POSSIBLE_CAST: Sensei, Rin, Kanna and the Public Safety/Public Peace Bureaus, the Kaiser General and his Spec Ops, RABBIT Squad, Momoka, Ayumu, Kaya [Vol 4-Vol F]

---

# SCHALE Office (Schale Tower)

DESC: The tower headquarters and residence hall of SCHALE, the Federal Investigation Club founded by the GSC President's secret directive; lavishly GSC-funded with money, ammunition, supplies, and helicopter assets, housing kitchens, a lounge, a theater, teaching rooms, and an Angel 24 convenience store staffed by the part-timer Sora; daily-rotating student representatives from each academy pass through its halls. [Prologue] the office sat derelict, occupied by delinquents until Sensei's arrival and the Wakamo incident cleared it, the Shittim Chest waiting inside; [Vol F: Ch 1] seized as the Kaiser coup's martial-law centerpiece, Rin imprisoned within, retaken in the joint operation of the RABBIT Squad and the Public Safety and Public Peace Bureaus, the General's Spec Ops wiped out in its corridors and his detonator shot from his hand; the SRT renegade FOX Platoon used the same chaos to steal an unidentified item from the building's basement, contents unrevealed; [Post-Vol F] restored to operations, Sensei's desk buried under the reconstruction's paperwork.

MAP: The office floor, the residence hall, Angel 24, the basement storage (site of the FOX Platoon's theft), the rooftop helicopter pad

NEARBY_LOC: District of Utnapishtim (its outskirts, roughly 30 km from the Sanctum Tower), Shiratori City

POSSIBLE_CAST: Sensei, Arona, Plana [Post-Vol F], Rin, Sora, rotating student representatives; [Vol F] Rin (prisoner), the Kaiser General, RABBIT Squad, Kanna, Momoka, Ayumu

---

# Abydos High School

DESC: The nearly-abandoned school on the eastern edge of the Abydos Desert District, once Kivotos' longest-running, financially and militarily powerful academy before desertification and ruinous debt hollowed it to five remaining students and its satellite, the Countermeasures Committee (the Foreclosure Task Force); carrying a debt of over 900 million yen (962,350,000) to Kaiser Loans, most of the district's land already sold. [Vol 1] the stage of the debt war: Helmet Gang attacks, the siege by Kaiser PMC forces, the Task Force's defense with Gehenna's and Trinity's aid, and the recovery of Hoshino; [Vol F] the Task Force sails from the district aboard the ancient weapon; [Post-Vol F] a second Shiroko quietly lives in the district as its scavenging guardian, listed on the student council's rolls.

MAP: The main building (old and new), the Countermeasures Committee club room, the school gate and courtyard, the Foreclosure Task Force's vegetable garden

NEARBY_LOC: Abydos Desert District (the parent region), the old Abydos ruins in the desert, the Kaiser excavation site, Kivotos' central districts (distant)

POSSIBLE_CAST: Takanashi Hoshino, Sunaookami Shiroko, Kuromi Serika, Izayoi Nonomi, Okusora Ayane; Sensei; Kuchinashi Yume [Pre-Story, deceased]; Problem Solver 68 [Vol 1 siege], the Prefect Team [Vol 1], Hifumi and Trinity reinforcements [Vol 1]; Sunaookami Shiroko Terror [Post-Vol F]

---

# Abydos Desert District

DESC: The vast desertified southern region surrounding Abydos High School, a former heartland consumed by advancing sands and sandstorm damage, most of its land sold to Kaiser by the old student council in futile debt service. [Vol 1] the Task Force's expedition through the remains of old Abydos and the discovery of Kaiser PMC's giant desert facility, the "board member" creditor's reveal; [Vol F: Ch 1] Kaiser PMC's excavation site discovers an ancient artifact beneath the sands; [Vol F: Ch 3] the site of the First False Sanctum, assaulted by the Foreclosure Task Force and Problem Solver 68 against the guardian Binah, and of the Task Force's seizure of the ancient weapon (Utnapishtim's Ship) from Kaiser's hands; [Pre-Story] Yume vanished and died within it. [Post-Vol F: Vol 1 Ch 3, JP] the Nephthys Group buys the district's debt bonds and the trans-desert railway question reopens the district's wounds.

MAP: The ruins of old Abydos (the buried original school and town), the Kaiser excavation site, the trans-desert railway (disused), the First Sanctum site [Vol F]

NEARBY_LOC: Abydos High School (the district's eastern edge), the Kivotos central districts (far north)

POSSIBLE_CAST: The Foreclosure Task Force, Problem Solver 68, Kaiser PMC forces, the Black Suit, the Helmet Gang remnants, Binah [Vol F: guardian]

---

# Gehenna Academy

DESC: The lawless mega-school of Kivotos, freedom and chaos institutionalized; a state unto itself whose order is maintained by the feared Prefect Team while its student council, the Pandemonium Society, schemes grand initiatives and its streets breed delinquents, gangs, and mercenary clubs like Problem Solver 68; sister-rival and treaty-partner of Trinity. [Vol 1] the Prefect Team's attempt to arrest PS68 overreaches into Abydos territory and ends in apology; [Vol 3] the Eden Treaty's Gehenna signatory: Makoto's Pandemonium Society commits to the ceremony, the Arius Squad's missiles strike the Society's and the Prefect Team's columns on the Day of Fire and Ash, and the Society's leadership is hospitalized; [Vol F: Ch 2] the district defense gathers every available prefect (Satsuki alone representing the Society, unhelpfully), the Society proper saving the Ninjutsu Research Club on the snowfield; a district without a dedicated main-story volume through Vol F, its arcs threaded through Vols 1, 3, and F.

MAP: The Prefect Team's headquarters, the Pandemonium Society's office, Problem Solver 68's office, the Gourmet Research Society's haunts, the School Lunch Club's kitchen, the Emergency Medicine Club, the Hot Springs Department

NEARBY_LOC: Trinity General School (rival, treaty-partner), the central districts, the Black Market (supply lines for its gangs)

POSSIBLE_CAST: Sorasaki Hina, Shiromi Iori, Amau Ako, Hinomiya Chinatsu, Hanuma Makoto, Natsume Iroha, Tanga Ibuki, Kyougoku Satsuki, Problem Solver 68 (Aru, Kayoko, Mutsuki, Haruka), the Gourmet Research Society, Fuuka, Juri, Sena, the Hot Springs Department, Sensei

---

# Trinity General School

DESC: The great school of order and faith, its campus a district of cathedrals, libraries, and gardens; internally a federation of powerful clubs (the Tea Party student council of three presidents, the Justice Task Force, the Sisterhood, the Knights Hospitaller, and countless more) with a centuries-long rivalry against Gehenna and a buried history of its own warring factions; the last bastion before the first Council banished Arius. [Vol 3] the Eden Treaty's Trinity signatory: the Make-Up Work Club's crisis, the traitor's hunt, the Tea Party's schism, the signing's destruction at the Old Cathedral of Communion, and the new ETO's appointment from Trinity's clubs; [Vol F: Ch 2] the district defense's largest civilian evacuation, organized by the Justice Task Force, the Library Committee, and the Vigilante Crew, with the Tea Party's help arriving unbidden and making things gloriously chaotic; Koharu's desperate fight near the ruins saved by Mika.

MAP: The Tea Party's salon, the Justice Task Force's citadel, the Sisterhood's chapel, the Make-Up Work Club's classroom, the annex (boot camp site), the old cathedral's approach, the school prison (Mika's cell), the catacombs' Trinity entrance

NEARBY_LOC: Gehenna Academy (rival, treaty-partner), the Old Cathedral of Communion, the Catacombs, the Arius District (via the underground catacombs' entrance)

POSSIBLE_CAST: The Tea Party (Nagisa, Mika, Seia), the Make-Up Work Club (Koharu, Azusa, Hifumi, Hanako), Hasumi and the Justice Task Force, the Sisterhood (Hinata, Mari, Sakurako), the Knights Hospitaller, the Library and Vigilante crews, the After-School Dessert Club, Sensei

---

# Trinity Catacombs (The Basilica)

DESC: The underground ossuary network beneath Trinity's holy district, its tunnels reaching as far as the Arius District's entrances; at its heart stands the Basilica, the church of the banished Arius schism and the site of Beatrice's ritual apparatus. [Vol 3: Ch 4] the Arius Squad's rescue route into the Arius District runs through the catacombs' entrance; [Vol F: Ch 2] the Fourth False Sanctum manifests at the Basilica in the Catacomb, assaulted by the Arius Squad, the Sisterhood, Hanako, and a tardy Sakurako still in Council attire, the guardian Hieronymus standing against them; [Pre-Story] the Saint Justina Council's bones and bequest rested here, and its resurrection during the Day of Fire and Ash rose from these depths.

MAP: The Basilica sanctuary, the ritual altar, the Council's ossuary, the Trinity-side entrance, the Arius-side exits

NEARBY_LOC: Trinity General School (above and adjacent), the Arius District (connected by the tunnels), the Old Cathedral of Communion

POSSIBLE_CAST: The Arius Squad, the Sisterhood, Hanako, Sakurako, the Knights Hospitaller, Beatrice [Vol 3], the Saint Justina Council and Saintess Barbara [Vol 3], Hieronymus [Vol F: guardian], Sensei

---

# Old Cathedral of Communion

DESC: The historic cathedral designated as the Eden Treaty's signing site, the symbolic bridge between Trinity and Gehenna; Hinata explains Trinity's history to Sensei in its halls before the ceremony, with Tsurugi's escort. [Vol 3: Ch 3] the signing day becomes the Day of Fire and Ash: the Arius Squad launches missiles at the departing Gehenna columns, Arius forces infiltrate the cathedral's basement under the chaos, the Justice Task Force and Hina battle the resurrected Saint Justina Council to protect Sensei, and Saori's attack on him fails by Hina and Sena's intervention; the treaty document is never signed here, its meaning rescued instead by Sensei's declaration of a new ETO; the cathedral and its surrounding district stand half-ruined after the fire.

MAP: The grand nave, the signing hall, the crypt-basement the Arius forces infiltrated, the surrounding plaza

NEARBY_LOC: Trinity General School, the Trinity Catacombs, Gehenna Academy (the columns' origin)

POSSIBLE_CAST: Sensei, Hasumi, Tsurugi, the Justice Task Force, Hina, Sena, Hinata, the Make-Up Work Club, the Arius Squad, the Saint Justina Council, Beatrice's forces

---

# Millennium Science School

DESC: The STEM superpower academy of Kivotos, its district a sprawl of laboratories, workshops, and ruins-adjacent campuses; governed by the Seminar (student council) under Rio's cold administration [Pre-Story through Vol 2], its culture a furnace of clubs: the Game Development Department, the Engineering Department, Veritas, C&C (the Maid Club), the Super Phenomenon Task Force, and more; three students in its history have achieved "Omniscience," Himari among them. [Vol 2] the stage of the G.BIBLE quest, Aris's awakening, the Eridu crisis, and Key's manifestation; [Vol F] the Engineering Department defends the district while an unknown AMAS observes without hostility, and the Seminar assaults the Fifth Sanctum at Eridu's outskirts.

MAP: The Seminar's council hall, the Game Development Department's clubroom (and locker room where Yuzu lives), the Engineering Department's workshops, Veritas' lab, the Maid Club's service wing, the contraband storage, the campus arcade

NEARBY_LOC: Millennium Ruins District, the Fortress City of Eridu, the central districts

POSSIBLE_CAST: Rio, Yuuka, Noa, Koyuki, the Game Development Department (Aris, Yuzu, Momoi, Midori), Himari, Eimi, Veritas, C&C, the Engineering Department, Sumire, Tendou Kei (Key), Sensei

---

# Millennium Ruins District

DESC: The abandoned industrial zone of Millennium's margins, ruins housing a shuttered munitions factory and the debris of older eras; the place Aris was found sleeping in a rundown factory's depths. [Vol 2] the Game Development Department's G.BIBLE quest fights hostile robots through these ruins to the factory where Aris slept; [Vol F: Ch 2] the Second False Sanctum manifests here, the assault force of C&C, Eimi, Tsurugi, and Ichika infiltrating the munitions factory while Chesed's endless Decagrammaton armies flood the field, Red Winter's reinforcements arriving to break the stalemate.

MAP: The abandoned munitions factory, the rundown factory (Aris's resting place), the G.BIBLE chamber, the Decagrammaton spawn fields [Vol F]

NEARBY_LOC: Millennium Science School, the Fortress City of Eridu, the central districts

POSSIBLE_CAST: The Game Development Department, C&C (Karin, Asuna, Akane, Neru), Eimi, Tsurugi, Ichika, Red Winter reinforcements, Chesed and the Decagrammaton armies [Vol F: guardians], Sensei

---

# Slumpia (Abandoned Amusement Park)

DESC: The derelict theme park closed by financial difficulties, its ferris wheel and coaster silhouettes a landmark of Kivotos' faded boom; the site's supplementary name "Slumpia" sticks in the records. [Vol F: Ch 2] the Third False Sanctum manifests over the park, the assault force of the RABBIT Squad, the Game Development Department, and Hina convening before its gates, "pulling out the big guns" with UZQueen deployed, against the twin guardians Shiro and Kuro.

MAP: The entrance plaza, the coaster loops, the ferris wheel, the carousel sanctum core [Vol F]

NEARBY_LOC: Millennium district, the D.U. margins, the central districts

POSSIBLE_CAST: RABBIT Squad, the Game Development Department, Hina, Shiro and Kuro [Vol F: guardians], Sensei

---

# Hyakkiyako Alliance Academy

DESC: The eastern district of yokai-inspired tradition, a federation of mystic clubs in place of a conventional school: the Yin-Yang Club serves as its administration (Niya the de facto president), the Hyakkaryouran Conflict Resolution Council its fixers, the Ninjutsu Research Department its shadows, the Training Club its mountain ascetics, and the Festival Management Committee its matsuri heart; founded in legend by the Great Prophet Kuzunoha. [Vol 5: Hyakkiyako, JP] the Council's collapse and restoration arc; [Vol F: Ch 2] the whole Allied Hyakkiyako Academy "bands together" in the district defense, the Ninjutsu Research Club dispatched on the snowfield special mission to find the Hyakkaryouran's leader, saved from the guardians' swarm by the Pandemonium Society's arrival from the skies.

MAP: The Yin-Yang Club's shrine-office, the Hyakkaryouran's hall, the Ninjutsu Research Department's dojo, the great snowfield, the festival grounds, the hot spring tourism quarter

NEARBY_LOC: Red Winter Federal Academy (fellow far district), the central districts, the mountain passes

POSSIBLE_CAST: Niya, Kaho, the Hyakkaryouran (Renge, Yukari, Kikyou, Nagusa), the Ninjutsu Research Club (Tsukuyo, Michiru, Izuna), Chise, the Training Club, Wakamo (suspended, at large), Kuzunoha (existence unconfirmed), the Pandemonium Society [Vol F], Sensei

---

# Red Winter Federal Academy

DESC: The perpetually revolutionary school-state of the far districts, a Soviet-flavored polity of ration queues, published coup schedules, and weekly revolutions that never remove President Cherino; administered by the Red Winter Office, guarded by the State Guard, powered by the Public Works Department's Labor Party, its underground press the Knowledge Liberation Front. [Vol F: Ch 2] the district defense mobilizes under Marina's State Guard and Minori's Labor Party members while Spec Ops No. 227 (Shigure, Nodoka) finds shelter and Cherino is nowhere to be found; [Vol F: Ch 2, Second Sanctum] Red Winter's reinforcements arrive at the Millennium Ruins to break Chesed's stalemate; no dedicated main-story volume through Vol F.

MAP: The Red Winter Office, the State Guard's barracks, the Public Works yards, the No. 227 Special Class, the Knowledge Liberation Front's press cellars, the frozen plaza

NEARBY_LOC: Hyakkiyako Alliance Academy, the central districts, the northern wastes

POSSIBLE_CAST: Cherino, Tomoe, Marina, Shigure, Nodoka, Minori, Meru, Momiji, the State Guard, the Labor Party, Sensei

---

# Shanhaijing Senior Secondary School

DESC: The Chinese-flavored school-state of the eastern merchant quarters, its power balanced between the Xuanlong Office (Genryumon, the student council under the "Master" Kisaki), the Plum Blossom Garden training-and-childcare department, the Chinese Alchemy Study Club, and the Xuanwu Merchant Association of the Gourmet District; a state of silks, ledgers, and strategic cuisine. [Vol F: Ch 2] the district defense mobilizes the Plum Blossom Garden, the Eastern Alchemy Society, and the Black Tortoise Promenade together, Kisaki taking action and sending Mina to the front; no dedicated main-story volume through Vol F.

MAP: The Xuanlong Office (Genryumon), the Plum Blossom Garden, the alchemy club's laboratory, the Gourmet District (the Xuanwu Merchant Association's territory), the opera house

NEARBY_LOC: Hyakkiyako Alliance Academy, the central districts, the trade roads

POSSIBLE_CAST: Kisaki, Mina, Shun, Kokona, Saya, Rumi, the Plum Blossom Garden's children, the merchant guilds, Sensei

---

# SRT Special Academy

DESC: The special forces academy of Kivotos, training elite squads (RABBIT, FOX, and their predecessors) under privilege-of-immunity operational doctrine; dissolved by the GSC's administrative decision before the story's curtain rises, its campus shuttered, its students scattered or radicalized.
